        #rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-grid button{position:relative;aspect-ratio:1/.83;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:2px;border:0;font-size:12px;color:var(--wish-muted)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-grid button.has{background:var(--wish-accent-soft);color:var(--wish-fg)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-grid button.is-selected{box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 2px var(--wish-accent)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-grid button span{font-size:8px;letter-spacing:1px;color:var(--wish-accent)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-grid button.is-live::after,#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-grid button.is-chosen::after{content:'';position:absolute;width:5px;height:5px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--wish-success);transform:translate(14px,-15px)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-grid button.is-chosen::after{background:var(--wish-accent)}
        #rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-legend{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;margin-top:12px;padding-top:9px;border-top:1px solid var(--wish-line);font-size:11px;color:var(--wish-muted)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-legend span:nth-child(2){color:var(--wish-success)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-legend span:nth-child(3){color:var(--wish-accent)}
        #rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-year-stats{margin:11px 4px;color:var(--wish-fg-2);font-size:12px}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-right h3{font-size:16px;margin:2px 0 12px}
        #rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-card{border:1px solid var(--wish-line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--wish-card);margin-bottom:8px;overflow:hidden}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-card summary{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:5px;padding:11px 13px;cursor:pointer}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-card summary strong{font-size:13px}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-card summary span{font-size:11px;color:var(--wish-accent-strong)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-card summary small{grid-column:1/-1;color:var(--wish-muted)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-card p{white-space:pre-wrap;line-height:1.65;margin:0;padding:10px 13px;color:var(--wish-fg-2);border-top:1px solid var(--wish-line)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-card>small{display:block;padding:0 13px 10px;color:var(--wish-muted)}
        #rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-other{margin-top:18px;border:1px dashed var(--wish-line);border-radius:10px}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-other>summary{padding:11px;cursor:pointer;color:var(--wish-fg-2)}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop [data-cal-other]{padding:0 10px}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-empty{color:var(--wish-muted);font-size:13px}
        @media(max-width:720px){#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-columns{display:flex;flex-direction:column;overflow:auto}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-left,#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-right{overflow:visible}#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop .rpcm-cal-right{border-left:0;border-top:1px solid var(--wish-line)}}
      `;document.head.appendChild(style);
    }
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop')?.remove();
    const rows=parseDatedLogBlocks((room.slots||[]).find(slot=>slot.id==='logSummary')?.content||'');
    const valid=row=>!row.isUnknown&&!row.isSpecialDate&&Number.isInteger(row.year)&&row.year>0&&Number.isInteger(row.month)&&Number.isInteger(row.day);
    const years=[...new Set(rows.filter(valid).map(row=>row.year))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
    const dated=rows.filter(valid).sort((a,b)=>a.year-b.year||a.month-b.month||a.day-b.day);
    const other=rows.filter(row=>!valid(row));
    const active=new Set(room.pending?activePendingItems(room.pending).filter(item=>item.group==='log-auto'||item.sourceSlotId==='logSummary').map(item=>String(item.sourceKey||'').replace(/^auto-log:/,'')):[]);
    const selected=new Set((room.manualLogSelectedKeys||[]).map(String));
    const pinned=new Set((room.autoLogPinnedKeys||[]).map(String));
    const candidates=new Set(room.autoLogRecallEnabled?selectTimelineAwareRecentLogBlocks(rows,room,recentLogInjectionCount(room)).map(row=>String(row.key)):[]);
    const status=row=>active.has(row.key)?'주입 중':pinned.has(row.key)?'항상 주입':selected.has(row.key)?'직접 선택':candidates.has(row.key)?'자동 후보':'보관 중';
    const card=row=>`<details class="rpcm-cal-card"><summary><strong>${esc(row.events||row.fullDate||'제목 없음')}</strong><span>${esc(status(row))}</span><small>${esc(row.fullDate||'날짜 미정')} · ${esc(String(row.body||'본문 없음').slice(0,110))}</small></summary><p>${esc(row.body||'본문 없음')}</p><small>${esc(row.timelineLabel||DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE)}</small></details>`;
    const backdrop=document.createElement('div');backdrop.id='rpcm-log-calendar-backdrop';
    backdrop.innerHTML=`<section class="rpcm-log-calendar" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="날짜로그 달력"><header><div><strong>날짜로그 달력</strong><span>저장된 날짜로그 ${rows.length}개 · CORE 달력의 탐색 방식</span></div><button type="button" data-cal-close aria-label="달력 닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">×</button></header><div class="rpcm-cal-columns"><div class="rpcm-cal-left"><div class="rpcm-cal-box"><div class="rpcm-cal-year"><button type="button" data-cal-prev aria-label="이전 연도">‹</button><select data-cal-year aria-label="연도 선택"></select><button type="button" data-cal-next aria-label="다음 연도">›</button><span></span><button type="button" data-cal-first>처음</button><button type="button" data-cal-last>최신</button></div><div class="rpcm-cal-months" data-cal-months></div><div class="rpcm-cal-week">${'일월화수목금토'.split('').map(day=>`<span>${day}</span>`).join('')}</div><div class="rpcm-cal-grid" data-cal-grid></div><div class="rpcm-cal-legend"><span>● 기록 양</span><span>● 주입 중</span><span>● 직접·항상 선택</span></div></div><div class="rpcm-cal-year-stats" data-cal-stats></div></div><div class="rpcm-cal-right"><h3 data-cal-heading></h3><div data-cal-cards></div><details class="rpcm-cal-other"><summary>정확한 연·월·일 외 기록 ${other.length}개</summary><div data-cal-other></div></details></div></div></section>`;
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
    const q=selector=>backdrop.querySelector(selector);
    const last=dated.at(-1),draft={year:last?.year||years.at(-1)||0,month:last?.month||1,day:last?.day||0};
    const dateRows=(year,month,day)=>dated.filter(row=>row.year===year&&row.month===month&&row.day===day);
    const chooseYear=(year,edge='last')=>{const subset=dated.filter(row=>row.year===year),row=edge==='first'?subset[0]:subset.at(-1);draft.year=year;draft.month=row?.month||1;draft.day=row?.day||0;render();};
    const render=()=>{
      q('[data-cal-year]').innerHTML=years.map(year=>`<option value="${year}" ${year===draft.year?'selected':''}>${year}년 · ${dated.filter(row=>row.year===year).length}개</option>`).join('');
      q('[data-cal-prev]').disabled=years.indexOf(draft.year)<=0;q('[data-cal-next]').disabled=years.indexOf(draft.year)>=years.length-1;
      const yearRows=dated.filter(row=>row.year===draft.year),monthRows=yearRows.filter(row=>row.month===draft.month);
      q('[data-cal-months]').innerHTML=Array.from({length:12},(_,i)=>{const month=i+1,has=yearRows.some(row=>row.month===month);return `<button type="button" data-cal-month="${month}" class="${month===draft.month?'is-selected':''}" ${has?'':'disabled'}>${month}</button>`;}).join('');
      if(!years.length){q('[data-cal-grid]').innerHTML='<p>연·월·일이 모두 적힌 날짜로그가 없습니다.</p>';q('[data-cal-heading]').textContent='날짜 기록 없음';q('[data-cal-cards]').innerHTML='';q('[data-cal-stats]').textContent='';q('.rpcm-cal-other').open=true;return;}
      const start=new Date(0);start.setUTCFullYear(draft.year,draft.month-1,1);const firstDay=start.getUTCDay();start.setUTCFullYear(draft.year,draft.month,0);const days=start.getUTCDate();
      q('[data-cal-grid]').innerHTML=Array.from({length:firstDay},()=>'<span aria-hidden="true"></span>').join('')+Array.from({length:days},(_,i)=>{const day=i+1,list=dateRows(draft.year,draft.month,day),has=list.length,live=list.some(row=>active.has(row.key)),chosen=list.some(row=>pinned.has(row.key)||selected.has(row.key)||candidates.has(row.key));return `<button type="button" data-cal-day="${day}" class="${has?'has':''} ${live?'is-live':chosen?'is-chosen':''} ${day===draft.day?'is-selected':''}" ${has?'':'disabled'} aria-label="${draft.year}년 ${draft.month}월 ${day}일 · 기록 ${has}개"><b>${day}</b><span>${'●'.repeat(Math.min(has,3))}</span></button>`;}).join('');
      q('[data-cal-stats]').textContent=`${draft.year}년 기록 ${new Set(yearRows.map(row=>`${row.month}-${row.day}`)).size}일 · 카드 ${yearRows.length}개 · 주입 중 ${yearRows.filter(row=>active.has(row.key)).length}개`;
      q('[data-cal-heading]').textContent=draft.day?`${draft.year}년 ${draft.month}월 ${draft.day}일 · ${dateRows(draft.year,draft.month,draft.day).length}개`:`${draft.year}년 ${draft.month}월`;
      q('[data-cal-cards]').innerHTML=dateRows(draft.year,draft.month,draft.day).map(card).join('')||'<p class="rpcm-cal-empty">이 날짜의 카드가 없습니다.</p>';
    };
    q('[data-cal-other]').innerHTML=other.map(row=>card(row)).join('')||'<p class="rpcm-cal-empty">다른 형식의 날짜 기록이 없습니다.</p>';
    q('[data-cal-close]').onclick=()=>backdrop.remove();backdrop.onclick=event=>{if(event.target===backdrop)backdrop.remove();};backdrop.onkeydown=event=>{if(event.key==='Escape')backdrop.remove();};
    q('[data-cal-year]').onchange=event=>chooseYear(Number(event.target.value));
    q('[data-cal-prev]').onclick=()=>chooseYear(years[years.indexOf(draft.year)-1]);q('[data-cal-next]').onclick=()=>chooseYear(years[years.indexOf(draft.year)+1]);
    q('[data-cal-first]').onclick=()=>chooseYear(years[0],'first');q('[data-cal-last]').onclick=()=>chooseYear(years.at(-1),'last');
    q('[data-cal-months]').onclick=event=>{const button=event.target.closest('[data-cal-month]');if(!button)return;draft.month=Number(button.dataset.calMonth);draft.day=dated.find(row=>row.year===draft.year&&row.month===draft.month)?.day||0;render();};
    q('[data-cal-grid]').onclick=event=>{const button=event.target.closest('[data-cal-day]');if(!button)return;draft.day=Number(button.dataset.calDay);render();};
    render();q('[data-cal-close]').focus();
  }

  function allImportantItemRawText(room) {
    return (room?.slots || []).filter(slot => slot.group === 'item').map(slot => {
      const title = cleanImportantItemTitle(slot.title || '제목 없음');
      const content = String(slot.content || '').trim();
      return '- **' + title + '**' + (content ? '\n' + content.split('\n').map(line => '  ' + line).join('\n') : '');
    }).join('\n\n');
  }

  function ui2TransientKey(node) {
    if (!node) return '';
    if (node.id) return `id:${node.id}`;
    const owner = node.closest('[data-inline-story-id],[data-person-record],[data-knowledge-id],[data-slot-id],[data-lore-pack],[data-lore-card],[data-person-name],[data-month-key],[data-pending-key],[id]');
    const ownerId = owner?.id || ['inlineStoryId','personRecord','knowledgeId','slotId','lorePack','loreCard','personName','monthKey','pendingKey'].map(name => owner?.dataset?.[name]).find(Boolean) || 'root';
    const field = node.dataset?.field || node.dataset?.personField || node.dataset?.inlineStoryAction || [...node.classList].filter(name => name.startsWith('rpcm-')).slice(0, 2).join('.') || node.tagName.toLowerCase();
    const peers = [...(node.parentElement?.children || [])].filter(peer => peer.tagName === node.tagName && (peer.dataset?.field || peer.dataset?.personField || peer.dataset?.inlineStoryAction || [...peer.classList].filter(name => name.startsWith('rpcm-')).slice(0, 2).join('.') || peer.tagName.toLowerCase()) === field);
    return `${ownerId}/${node.tagName.toLowerCase()}:${field}:${peers.indexOf(node)}`;
  }

  function ui2ControlBaseline(node) {
    if (node.type === 'checkbox' || node.type === 'radio') return String(node.defaultChecked);
    if (node.tagName === 'SELECT') return String([...node.options].find(option => option.defaultSelected)?.value || '');
    return String(node.defaultValue || '');
  }

  function captureUi2TransientState(overlay, room) {
    if (isMobileManagerLayout() || !overlay?.querySelector('#rpcm-modal')) return null;
    const details = new Map();
    overlay.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-modal details').forEach(node => details.set(ui2TransientKey(node), node.open));
    const controls = new Map();
    overlay.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-modal :is(input:not([type=checkbox]):not([type=radio]):not([type=file]):not([type=hidden]),textarea)').forEach(node => {
      if (node.readOnly) return;
      controls.set(ui2TransientKey(node), { value:String(node.value || ''), baseline:ui2ControlBaseline(node) });
    });
    const scrolls = new Map();
    overlay.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-modal :is(textarea,.rpcm-read-preview,.rpcm-item-read-preview,.rpcm-inline-story-list,.rpcm-person-profiles)').forEach(node => {
      if (node.scrollTop > 0 || node.scrollLeft > 0) scrolls.set(ui2TransientKey(node), [node.scrollTop, node.scrollLeft]);
    });
    const draftPanels = [...overlay.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-modal :is(.rpcm-inline-story-edit,.rpcm-knowledge-fields,.rpcm-person-fields):not([hidden])')].map(panel => ({
      key:ui2TransientKey(panel),
      fields:[...panel.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea')].map(field => ({ key:ui2TransientKey(field), value:field.value, baseline:ui2ControlBaseline(field), checked:field.type === 'checkbox' ? field.checked : null })),
    }));
    const childEditors = [...overlay.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-modal .rpcm-item-child-editor')].map(editor => ({
      slotId:editor.closest('[data-slot-id]')?.dataset.slotId || '',
      index:editor.closest('.rpcm-item-child')?.querySelector('[data-item-child-edit]')?.dataset.itemChildEdit || '',
      slotBaseline:String(editor.closest('.rpcm-slot')?.querySelector('.rpcm-textarea')?.defaultValue || ''),
      name:editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-name')?.value || '', keeper:editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-keeper')?.value || '', description:editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-description')?.value || '',
    }));
    const active = overlay.contains(document.activeElement) ? document.activeElement : null;
    let selection = null;
    try { if (active && typeof active.selectionStart === 'number') selection = [active.selectionStart, active.selectionEnd]; } catch (_) {}
    return {
      roomId:String(room.chatId || ''), details, controls, scrolls, draftPanels, childEditors,
      focusKey:active ? ui2TransientKey(active) : '', selection,
      searchOpen:overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-header-search-pop')?.hidden === false,
      searchResultsOpen:overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-search-results')?.hidden === false,
      searchIndex:overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-search-input')?.dataset.searchIndex || '',
      headerMenuOpen:!!overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-header-menu[open]'),
    };
  }

  function restoreUi2TransientState(overlay, room, snapshot) {
    if (!snapshot || snapshot.roomId !== String(room.chatId || '') || isMobileManagerLayout()) return;
    const keyed = selector => new Map([...overlay.querySelectorAll(selector)].map(node => [ui2TransientKey(node), node]));
    const details = keyed('#rpcm-modal details');
    snapshot.details.forEach((open, key) => { const node = details.get(key); if (node) node.open = open; });
    for (const draft of snapshot.childEditors) {
      if (!draft.slotId || draft.index === '') continue;
      const slot = [...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-slot[data-slot-id]')].find(node => node.dataset.slotId === draft.slotId);
      if (String(slot?.querySelector('.rpcm-textarea')?.defaultValue || '') !== draft.slotBaseline) continue;
      const button = [...(slot?.querySelectorAll('[data-item-child-edit]') || [])].find(node => node.dataset.itemChildEdit === draft.index);
      if (!button) continue;
      button.click();
      const editor = button.closest('.rpcm-item-child')?.querySelector('.rpcm-item-child-editor');
      if (!editor) continue;
      editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-name').value = draft.name;
      editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-keeper').value = draft.keeper;
      editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-description').value = draft.description;
    }
    const panels = keyed('#rpcm-modal :is(.rpcm-inline-story-edit,.rpcm-knowledge-fields,.rpcm-person-fields)');
    for (const draft of snapshot.draftPanels) {
      const panel = panels.get(draft.key);
      if (!panel) continue;
      const fields = new Map([...panel.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea')].map(node => [ui2TransientKey(node), node]));
      if (draft.fields.some(value => { const field=fields.get(value.key); return field && ui2ControlBaseline(field) !== value.baseline; })) continue;
      panel.hidden = false;
      const read = panel.parentElement?.querySelector(':scope > :is(.rpcm-inline-story-read,.rpcm-knowledge-read,.rpcm-person-read)');
      if (read) read.hidden = true;
      for (const value of draft.fields) {
        const field = fields.get(value.key);
        if (!field) continue;
        if (value.checked !== null) field.checked = value.checked;
        else field.value = value.value;
      }
    }
    const controls = keyed('#rpcm-modal :is(input:not([type=checkbox]):not([type=radio]):not([type=file]):not([type=hidden]),textarea)');
    snapshot.controls.forEach(({ value, baseline }, key) => { const node = controls.get(key); if (node && !node.readOnly && ui2ControlBaseline(node) === baseline && node.value !== value) node.value = value; });
    const searchPop = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-header-search-pop');
    const searchToggle = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-header-search-toggle');
    if (searchPop && searchToggle && snapshot.searchOpen) {
      searchPop.hidden = false;
      searchToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
      searchToggle.setAttribute('aria-label', '통합 검색 닫기');
      searchToggle.innerHTML = rpcmUiIcon('close');
    }
    const search = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-search-input');
    if (search?.value) {
      if (snapshot.searchIndex !== '') search.dataset.restoreIndex = snapshot.searchIndex;
      search.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles:true }));
      if (!snapshot.searchOpen || !snapshot.searchResultsOpen) overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-search-results').hidden = true;
    }
    const menu = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-header-menu');
    if (menu && !snapshot.searchOpen) menu.open = snapshot.headerMenuOpen;
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      if (state.modal !== overlay || state.currentRoom !== room) return;
      const scrollers = keyed('#rpcm-modal :is(textarea,.rpcm-read-preview,.rpcm-item-read-preview,.rpcm-inline-story-list,.rpcm-person-profiles)');
      snapshot.scrolls.forEach(([top, left], key) => { const node = scrollers.get(key); if (node) { node.scrollTop = top; node.scrollLeft = left; } });
      const focus = keyed('#rpcm-modal :is(input,textarea,select,button,summary)').get(snapshot.focusKey);
      if (focus && isElementVisible(focus)) {
        try { focus.focus({ preventScroll:true }); } catch (_) { focus.focus(); }
        if (snapshot.selection && typeof focus.setSelectionRange === 'function') { try { focus.setSelectionRange(...snapshot.selection); } catch (_) {} }
      }
    });
  }


  // CORE 1.0.6's single anchored help popover, adapted to Wish colors and view names.

    recall:['날짜로그 회수','활성 시간선의 최근 날짜로그와 관련 로그를 설정한 개수만큼 용량 안에서 선택합니다. 직접 선택과 제외는 기억 화면에서 관리합니다.'],
    context:['AI 맥락 검토','관련 날짜로그 후보를 API로 다시 검토합니다. 실패하면 기본 검색을 사용합니다. 현재 방 설정입니다.'],
    guide:['지침','현재 방의 사용자 수정 지침을 우선합니다. 지침 보기나 복사는 저장값을 변경하지 않습니다.'],
  };
  function rpcmHelpSections(key) {
    const sections={
      update:[['생성','완료된 USER+AI 턴만 작업자료에 넣습니다. 범위를 직접 지정해 이미 처리한 턴도 재검토할 수 있습니다.'],['검토·저장','결과를 먼저 파싱하고 Diff를 보여줍니다. 사용자 확인과 로컬 저장 성공 후에만 해당 수동 기준점이 이동합니다.']],
      ai:[['AI 기억정리','여섯 영역의 자동 생성과 자동 반영을 각각 켜고 주기를 정합니다. 자동 반영은 이후 생성한 유효 결과에만 적용하며, 기존 초안은 직접 검토합니다. 수동 작업 대기 중에는 자동 작업이 기다립니다.'],['사용량','호출 시 입력·출력 사용량이 기록됩니다. 실패한 결과는 기억에 저장하지 않습니다.']],
      recall:[['연속성','활성 시간선의 최근 날짜로그를 우선합니다.'],['관련 로그','관련도 기준과 남은 용량을 통과한 후보만 추가합니다.']],
      preferences:[['범위','AI 연결은 이 기기, 기억 회수와 AI 기억정리는 현재 방에 적용됩니다.'],['저장','토글과 주기를 바꾸면 해당 저장소에 즉시 기록합니다.']],
      lore:[['소유권','사용자 관리·잠금 자료는 AI 결과가 바꾸지 않습니다.'],['주입','카드의 포함 방식과 상세·압축 본문은 기억 회수 단계에서 선택합니다.']],
    };
    return sections[key]||[];
  }
  function rpcmHelpButton(key) {
    if (!RPCM_HELP[key]) return '';
    return `<button type="button" class="rpcm-core-help" data-rpcm-help="${key}" aria-label="${esc(RPCM_HELP[key][0])} 도움말" aria-expanded="false" aria-describedby="rpcm-core-tip">?</button>`;
  }
  let rpcmTipAnchor = null, rpcmTipPinned = false;
  function rpcmHideTip() {
    rpcmTipAnchor?.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
    rpcmTipAnchor = null; rpcmTipPinned = false;
    document.getElementById('rpcm-core-tip')?.remove();
  }
  function rpcmShowTip(button, pinned = false) {
    const detail = RPCM_HELP[button?.dataset?.rpcmHelp];
    if (!detail) return;
    if (rpcmTipAnchor !== button) rpcmHideTip();
    rpcmTipAnchor = button; rpcmTipPinned = pinned;
    const tip = document.getElementById('rpcm-core-tip') || document.createElement('aside');
    tip.id='rpcm-core-tip';tip.className='rpcm-core-tip';tip.setAttribute('role','tooltip');
    tip.innerHTML=`<strong>${esc(detail[0])}</strong><p>${esc(detail[1])}</p>${rpcmHelpSections(button.dataset.rpcmHelp).map(([title,body])=>`<section><h4>${esc(title)}</h4><p>${esc(body)}</p></section>`).join('')}`;
    if (!tip.isConnected) document.body.appendChild(tip);
    const theme=getComputedStyle(state.modal||button);
    for(const token of ['--wish-card','--wish-fg','--wish-fg-2','--wish-line','--wish-accent-line','--wish-accent-strong']){
      const value=theme.getPropertyValue(token).trim();if(value)tip.style.setProperty(token,value);
    }
    tip.onpointerleave=()=>{if(!rpcmTipPinned)rpcmHideTip();};
    button.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');
    const rect=button.getBoundingClientRect(), width=Math.min(350,window.innerWidth-24);
    tip.style.width=`${width}px`;
    const x=Math.max(12,Math.min(window.innerWidth-width-12,rect.left-12));
    const height=tip.getBoundingClientRect().height;
    const y=rect.bottom+8+height>window.innerHeight-12 ? Math.max(12,rect.top-height-8) : rect.bottom+8;
    tip.style.left=`${x}px`;tip.style.top=`${y}px`;
  }
  function bindRpcmHelp(overlay) {
    for (const [id,key] of Object.entries({'rpcm-section-overview':'overview','rpcm-section-basic':'memory','rpcm-section-item':'items','rpcm-section-people':'people','rpcm-section-lore':'lore','rpcm-section-timeline':'timeline','rpcm-section-update':'update','rpcm-section-longterm':'longterm','rpcm-section-ai':'ai','rpcm-section-preferences':'preferences'})) {
      const heading=overlay.querySelector(`#${id} h2`);
      if (heading && !heading.querySelector('.rpcm-core-help')) heading.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',rpcmHelpButton(key));
    }
    if (overlay._rpcmCoreHelpBound) return;
    overlay._rpcmCoreHelpBound=true;
    overlay.addEventListener('pointerover',event=>{const button=event.target.closest?.('[data-rpcm-help]');if(button && event.pointerType!=='touch' && !rpcmTipPinned)rpcmShowTip(button);});
    overlay.addEventListener('pointerout',event=>{if(rpcmTipAnchor && !rpcmTipPinned && !rpcmTipAnchor.contains(event.relatedTarget) && !document.getElementById('rpcm-core-tip')?.contains(event.relatedTarget))rpcmHideTip();});
    overlay.addEventListener('focusin',event=>{const button=event.target.closest?.('[data-rpcm-help]');if(button && !rpcmTipPinned)rpcmShowTip(button);});
    overlay.addEventListener('focusout',event=>{if(rpcmTipAnchor && !rpcmTipPinned && !rpcmTipAnchor.contains(event.relatedTarget))rpcmHideTip();});
    overlay.addEventListener('click',event=>{const button=event.target.closest?.('[data-rpcm-help]');if(!button)return;event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();if(rpcmTipAnchor===button&&rpcmTipPinned)rpcmHideTip();else rpcmShowTip(button,true);},true);
    overlay.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Escape')rpcmHideTip();});
  }

  // CORE's confirmed-turn scheduling is adapted to six Wish outputs. No CORE data schema is stored.
  const AI_MEMORY_AREAS = Object.freeze({
    currentState:{label:'현재상태',manual:'currentItems',interval:10},
    logSummary:{label:'날짜로그',manual:'logSummary',interval:10},
    importantItems:{label:'중요 물품',manual:'currentItems',interval:10},
    personInfo:{label:'인물정보',manual:'personInfo',interval:10},
    lore:{label:'자료집',manual:'lore',interval:10},
    timeline:{label:'타임라인',manual:'timeline',interval:10},
  });
  const aiMemoryRuntime = {busy:false,roomId:'',lastPoll:new Map(),lastCounts:new Map()};
  function aiMemoryConfig(room) {
    if (!room.aiMemoryAutomation || typeof room.aiMemoryAutomation !== 'object') room.aiMemoryAutomation={enabled:false,areas:{},undo:[]};
    const config=room.aiMemoryAutomation;
    config.enabled=config.enabled===true;
    config.applyRevision=Number(config.applyRevision)||0;
    if (!config.areas || typeof config.areas!=='object') config.areas={};
    if (!Array.isArray(config.undo)) config.undo=[];
    for(const [key,meta] of Object.entries(AI_MEMORY_AREAS)) {
      const row=config.areas[key] && typeof config.areas[key]==='object' ? config.areas[key] : {};
      row.enabled=row.enabled===true;
      row.autoApply=row.autoApply===true;
      row.applyRevision=Number(row.applyRevision)||0;
      row.interval=Math.max(1,Math.min(100,Number(row.interval)||meta.interval));
      row.cursor=String(row.cursor||'');
      row.baseline=String(row.baseline||'');
      row.appliedAt=String(row.appliedAt||'');
      row.error=String(row.error||'');
      row.failedEnd=String(row.failedEnd||'');
      row.draft=row.draft && typeof row.draft==='object' ? row.draft : null;
      row.status=String(row.status||'대기');
      config.areas[key]=row;
    }
    return config;
  }
  function aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(room,area) {
    const slots=room.slots||[];
    if(area==='currentState')return JSON.stringify(slots.find(slot=>slot.id==='currentState')||null);
    if(area==='importantItems')return JSON.stringify(slots.filter(slot=>slot.group==='item'));
    if(area==='logSummary')return JSON.stringify({slot:slots.find(slot=>slot.id==='logSummary')||null,timeline:activeLogTimelineLabel(room),pinned:room.autoLogPinnedKeys||[],selected:room.manualLogSelectedKeys||[]});
    if(area==='personInfo')return JSON.stringify({knowledge:room.knowledgeCards||[],records:room.personStateRecords||[]});
    if(area==='lore')return JSON.stringify(lorePacksFor(room));
    return JSON.stringify({cards:room.storyTimelineCards||[],reviewed:room.storyTimelineLastReviewedMessageId||'',label:activeLogTimelineLabel(room)});
  }
  function aiMemoryEffectiveCursor(room,area,turns) {
    const task=AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual;
    const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];
    const manual=manualCheckpointForTask(room,task).endMessageId;
    const keys=[row.cursor||row.baseline,manual===aiMemoryIgnoredManualAnchor(room,area)?"":manual].filter(Boolean);
    let index=-1,key='';
    for(const candidate of keys){const found=rpcmFindTurnIndex(turns,candidate);if(found<0)throw new Error(`${AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label}: 기준 턴 ID를 현재 기록에서 찾지 못했습니다. 메시지 ID 변경·리롤·분기 가능성을 확인해 주세요.`);if(found>index){index=found;key=candidate;}}
    return {index,key};
  }
  function aiMemoryPending(room,area,turns) {
    const {index,key}=aiMemoryEffectiveCursor(room,area,turns);
    return {count:Math.max(0,turns.length-index-1),index,key};
  }
  function aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,key){
    if(!key)return '없음';
    const keys=manualUpdateRuntime.counts.get(String(room.chatId||''))?.turnKeys||[];
    const index=keys.indexOf(String(key));
    return index>=0?`완료 RP ${index+1}턴`:'기준점 저장됨';
  }
  function aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room) {
    const overlay=state.modal;
    if(!overlay||state.currentRoom!==room)return;
    const config=aiMemoryConfig(room),counts=aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.get(String(room.chatId))||{};
    for(const [area,row] of Object.entries(config.areas)){
      const node=overlay.querySelector(`[data-ai-memory-area="${area}"]`);
      if(!node)continue;
      const count=counts[area];
      const label=`${aiMemoryProgressLabel(room,area)} · 마지막 AI 적용 ${aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,row.cursor)} · ${row.status} · ${row.autoApply?'자동 반영 ON':'검토 후 적용'}${row.autoApplyError?` · 보류 이유 ${row.autoApplyError.slice(0,90)}`:''}${row.draft?` · 검토 대기 ${row.draft.turnCount}턴`:''}${row.draft?.sharedCallId?' · 현재상태·물품 공유 호출 1회':''}${row.plannedStart?` · 예정 범위 ${aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,row.plannedStart)}~${aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,row.plannedEnd)}`:''}${row.error?` · 실패 ${row.error.slice(0,90)}`:''}`;
      const small=node.querySelector('small');if(small)small.textContent=label;
      const button=node.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-review]');if(button)button.disabled=!row.draft;
      const applyToggle=node.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-auto-apply]');if(applyToggle)applyToggle.checked=row.autoApply;
      const hasUndo=config.undo.some(entry=>entry.area===area),undo=node.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-undo]');
      if(hasUndo&&!undo){
        const action=document.createElement('button');action.type='button';action.className='rpcm-btn secondary';action.dataset.aiMemoryUndo=area;action.textContent='직전 적용 되돌리기';
        action.onclick=async()=>{action.disabled=true;try{await aiMemoryUndo(room,area);renderModalIfOpen();notify('AI 적용 내용과 해당 항목 기준점을 함께 되돌렸습니다.','success');}catch(error){notify(`되돌리기 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}finally{if(action.isConnected)action.disabled=false;}};
        node.querySelector('div')?.append(action);
      }else if(!hasUndo&&undo)undo.remove();
    }
    const shortcut=overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-ai-memory-shortcut span');
    if(shortcut)shortcut.textContent=`${config.enabled?'자동 생성 ON':'자동 생성 OFF'} · 검토 대기 ${Object.values(config.areas).filter(row=>row.draft).length}개`;
    const overview=overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-ai-memory-overview');
    if(overview){const summary=overview.querySelector('p');if(summary)summary.textContent=`자동 생성 ${config.enabled?'ON':'OFF'} · 검토 대기 ${Object.values(config.areas).filter(row=>row.draft).length}개`;overview.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-area-toggle]').forEach(node=>{const area=node.dataset.aiAreaToggle,row=config.areas[area],on=config.enabled&&row.enabled;node.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(on));node.querySelector('em').textContent=on?'ON':'OFF';node.querySelector('span').textContent=row.draft?'초안 검토 대기':row.error?'확인 필요':on?'자동 생성 사용 중':'자동 생성 꺼짐';node.querySelector('.rpcm-ai-collect-status').textContent=aiMemoryProgressLabel(room,area);});}
  }

  function aiMemorySummary(room) {
    const config=aiMemoryConfig(room), counts=aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.get(String(room.chatId))||{};
    return `<article class="rpcm-pref-card rpcm-ai-memory-settings"><header><h3>AI 기억정리 ${rpcmHelpButton('ai')}</h3><span class="rpcm-pref-scope">현재 방 · 자동 호출 기본 OFF</span></header><p>완료된 USER+AI 턴을 항목별 주기에 따라 분석합니다. 기본은 초안 검토 후 적용입니다. 항목별 자동 반영을 켜면 원문·형식·저장본 검증을 통과한 새 결과를 바로 저장합니다. 충돌 시 초안으로 보류합니다.</p>
      <label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>자동 생성 전체 사용<small>OFF면 자동 검사·오류 알림 중지 · 기존 자료·초안 유지</small></span><input type="checkbox" data-ai-memory-global ${config.enabled?'checked':''}></label>
      ${Object.entries(AI_MEMORY_AREAS).map(([key,meta])=>{const row=config.areas[key],count=counts[key];const draft=row.draft;return `<div class="rpcm-ai-memory-row" data-ai-memory-area="${key}"><label><input type="checkbox" data-ai-memory-enable="${key}" ${row.enabled?'checked':''}><strong>${meta.label}</strong></label><label><input type="checkbox" data-ai-memory-auto-apply="${key}" ${row.autoApply?'checked':''}> 자동 반영</label><label>주기 <input type="number" min="1" max="100" step="1" data-ai-memory-interval="${key}" value="${row.interval}"> 턴</label><small>${esc(aiMemoryProgressLabel(room,key))} · 마지막 적용 ${esc(aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,row.cursor))}${row.baseline?` · 시작점 ${esc(aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,row.baseline))}`:''}${draft?` · 검토 대기 ${draft.turnCount}턴`:''}${draft?.sharedCallId?' · 현재상태·물품 공유 호출 1회':''} · ${esc(row.status)} · ${row.autoApply?'자동 반영 ON':'검토 후 적용'}${row.autoApplyError?` · 보류 이유 ${esc(row.autoApplyError.slice(0,150))}`:''}${row.plannedStart?` · 예정 ${esc(aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,row.plannedStart))}~${esc(aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,row.plannedEnd))}`:''}${row.lastUsage?` · 최근 입력 ${formatCount(row.lastUsage.inputTokens||0)} / 출력 ${formatCount(row.lastUsage.outputTokens||0)} tokens`:''}${row.error?` · 실패 ${esc(row.error.slice(0,90))}`:''}</small><div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-ai-memory-run="${key}">바로 실행</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-ai-memory-review="${key}" ${draft?'':'disabled'}>초안 검토</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-ai-memory-baseline="${key}">시작점</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-ai-memory-now="${key}">지금부터</button>${config.undo.some(entry=>entry.area===key)?`<button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-ai-memory-undo="${key}">직전 적용 되돌리기</button>`:''}</div></div>`;}).join('')}
      <p>자동·수동 전환은 기존 기억·초안·기준점을 유지합니다. 자동 작업은 수동으로 저장한 마지막 구간 다음부터 이어갑니다. 수동 범위 지정으로 이미 처리한 턴도 다시 검토할 수 있습니다. 자동 반영 OFF는 생성까지 끄지 않습니다.</p></article>`;
  }
  function aiMemoryOverview(room) {
    if(!room)return '';
    const config=aiMemoryConfig(room),counts=aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.get(String(room.chatId))||{};
    return `<header><h3>AI 기억정리 ${rpcmHelpButton('ai')}</h3><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-ai-memory-open-settings>항목별 설정·검토</button></header><p>자동 생성 ${config.enabled?'ON':'OFF'} · 검토 대기 ${Object.values(config.areas).filter(row=>row.draft).length}개 · 카드를 길게 누르면 해당 설정으로 이동</p><div class="rpcm-ai-overview-grid">${Object.entries(AI_MEMORY_AREAS).map(([area,meta])=>{const row=config.areas[area],draft=row.draft,range=row.lastAppliedStart&&row.lastAppliedEnd?`${esc(aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,row.lastAppliedStart))} ~ ${esc(aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,row.lastAppliedEnd))}`:'없음';return `<button type="button" class="rpcm-ai-area-toggle" data-ai-area-toggle="${area}" aria-pressed="${config.enabled&&row.enabled}" aria-label="${esc(meta.label)} 자동 생성 ${config.enabled&&row.enabled?'켜짐':'꺼짐'} · 짧게 누르면 ON/OFF, 길게 누르면 설정" title="짧게 누르면 ON/OFF · 길게 누르면 ${esc(meta.label)} 설정"><strong>${meta.label}<em>${config.enabled&&row.enabled?'ON':'OFF'}</em></strong><span>${draft?'초안 검토 대기':row.error?'실패·주의':config.enabled&&row.enabled?'자동 ON':'자동 OFF'}${counts[area]!=null?` · 대기 ${counts[area]}턴`:''}</span><small>AI 생성 초안 ${draft?`${draft.turnCount}턴 · ${esc(aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,draft.startKey))} ~ ${esc(aiMemoryTurnLabel(room,draft.endKey))}`:'없음'} · ${row.autoApply?'자동 반영':'검토 후 적용'} ${range}</small><small class="rpcm-ai-collect-status">${esc(aiMemoryProgressLabel(room,area))}</small></button>`;}).join('')}</div>`;
  }

  function openAiMemoryAreaSettings(overlay,area) {
    if(!overlay||!AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area])return;
    showWorkView(overlay,'tools',true);
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      if(!overlay.isConnected)return;
      const row=overlay.querySelector(`[data-ai-memory-area="${area}"]`);
      if(!row)return;
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-ai-memory-row.rpcm-ai-memory-focus').forEach(node=>node.classList.remove('rpcm-ai-memory-focus'));
      row.classList.add('rpcm-ai-memory-focus');
      row.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'});
      const focus=row.querySelector(`[data-ai-memory-interval="${area}"]`)||row.querySelector('input,button');
      focus?.focus?.({preventScroll:true});
      setTimeout(()=>row.isConnected&&row.classList.remove('rpcm-ai-memory-focus'),1800);
    }));
  }
  function aiMemoryHistoryForPacket(room,task,startIndex,endIndex,turns=[]) {
    const areas=task==='currentItems'?['currentState','importantItems']:[task];
    const rows=areas.map(area=>{const row=room?.aiMemoryAutomation?.areas?.[area]||{};const aiStart=rpcmFindTurnIndex(turns,String(row.lastAppliedStart||'')),aiEnd=rpcmFindTurnIndex(turns,String(row.lastAppliedEnd||''));const overlap=aiStart>=0&&aiEnd>=aiStart&&Math.max(startIndex,aiStart)<=Math.min(endIndex,aiEnd)?`${turns[Math.max(startIndex,aiStart)].key}..${turns[Math.min(endIndex,aiEnd)].key}`:'없음';return `${area}: AI_APPLIED_RANGE=${row.lastAppliedStart&&row.lastAppliedEnd?`${row.lastAppliedStart}..${row.lastAppliedEnd}`:'없음'}; AI_LAST_APPLIED=${row.cursor||'없음'}; MANUAL_LAST_APPLIED=${manualCheckpointForTask(room,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual).endMessageId||'없음'}; AI_BASELINE=${row.baseline||'없음'}; AI_DRAFT=${row.draft?`${row.draft.startKey}..${row.draft.endKey} (미적용)`:'없음'}; AI_OVERLAP_RANGE=${overlap}`;});
    return `[AI_MEMORY_HISTORY]\n${rows.join('\n')}\n이번 수동 범위 인덱스=${startIndex+1}..${endIndex+1}. 이전 AI 답변·미적용 초안은 사실 근거가 아닙니다. 근거는 이번 패킷의 RP 원문·사용자 메모·명시된 기존 저장본뿐입니다.\n[/AI_MEMORY_HISTORY]`;
  }
  async function aiMemoryAssertSource(room,draft) {
    if(draft.stale)throw new Error(draft.staleReason||'원문 변경으로 재검토가 필요합니다.');
    if(state.currentRoom!==room || String(room.chatId)!==draft.roomId)throw new Error('방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    const loaded=draft.historyStartKey?await loadRpcmCompletedRange(room,[draft.historyStartKey,draft.endKey]):await loadManualCompletedTurns(room);
    const from=draft.historyStartKey?rpcmFindTurnIndex(loaded,draft.historyStartKey):0;
    if(from<0)throw new Error('초안 시작 원문이 사라졌습니다. 다시 생성해 주세요.');
    const turns=loaded.slice(from);
    const prefix=turns.slice(0,draft.endIndex+1).map(turn=>[String(turn.key),String(turn.text)]);
    if(prefix.length!==draft.endIndex+1 || JSON.stringify(prefix)!==JSON.stringify(draft.historyPrefix))throw new Error('RP 원문·리롤·분기가 바뀌었습니다. 이 초안은 적용할 수 없습니다.');
    return turns;
  }
  function aiMemoryComparableSnapshot(snapshot,area) {
    const data=typeof snapshot==='string'?JSON.parse(snapshot):structuredClone(snapshot);
    const cleanSlot=slot=>{
      if(!slot)return slot;
      const next={...slot};
      for(const key of ['enabled','lastAutoMatch','lastAutoConfidence','lastAutoReason','lastAutoDetectedAt'])delete next[key];
      return next;
    };
    if(area==='importantItems')return JSON.stringify((data||[]).map(cleanSlot));
    if(area==='currentState')return JSON.stringify(cleanSlot(data));
    return JSON.stringify(data);
  }
  async function aiMemoryAssertDraft(room,area,draft) {
    await aiMemoryAssertSource(room,draft);
    if(aiMemoryComparableSnapshot(aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(room,area),area)!==aiMemoryComparableSnapshot(draft.areaSnapshot,area))throw new Error('이 초안의 기억 본문 또는 사용자 설정이 변경됐습니다. 새 저장본과 비교해 다시 생성해 주세요.');
    if(simpleHash(userCanonMemoBlock(room))!==draft.memoHash)throw new Error('USER_CANON_MEMO가 바뀌었습니다. 다시 생성해 주세요.');
    if(manualGuideMetadata(room,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual).hash!==draft.guideHash)throw new Error('지침이 바뀌었습니다. 다시 생성해 주세요.');
    if(JSON.stringify(manualCheckpointForTask(room,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual))!==JSON.stringify(draft.packetDraft.checkpoint))throw new Error('수동 적용 기준점이 바뀌었습니다. 오래된 AI 초안을 적용하지 않습니다.');
  }
  function aiMemoryOutputValues(area,raw,draft=null) {
    if(area==='currentState'||area==='importantItems'){
      if(draft?.sharedCurrentItems){
        // Each shared call produces two separately editable drafts. Keep the other
        // output only as parser context; it is never applied by this area's draft.
        return area==='currentState'
          ? {currentState:String(raw||''),importantItems:String(draft.sharedOtherOutput||'')}
          : {currentState:String(draft.sharedOtherOutput||''),importantItems:String(raw||'')};
      }
      const text=String(raw||'').trim().replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*\n|\n```\s*$/g,'');let object;
      try{object=JSON.parse(text);}catch(_){throw new Error('현재상태·중요물품 AI 결과는 두 문자열을 가진 JSON이어야 합니다.');}
      if(!object||typeof object.currentState!=='string'||typeof object.importantItems!=='string')throw new Error('currentState / importantItems 전체본이 모두 필요합니다.');
      return {currentState:object.currentState,importantItems:object.importantItems};
    }
    return {result:String(raw||'')};
  }
  function aiMemoryPrepare(room,area,draft,raw) {
    const task=AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual;
    let parseRoom=room;
    if(draft.sharedCurrentItems){
      // The manual parser validates the combined currentItems existing result.
      // Recreate that input from generation time so applying either sibling first
      // does not make the still untouched sibling's draft stale.
      parseRoom=structuredClone(room);
      parseRoom.slots=(parseRoom.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.id!=='currentState'&&slot.group!=='item')
        .concat(structuredClone(draft.sharedCurrentItemsSlots||[]));
    }
    const parserDraft=(area==='currentState'||area==='importantItems')
      ? {...draft.packetDraft,existing:manualExistingResult(parseRoom,task)} : draft.packetDraft;
    const prepared=prepareManualUpdateResult(parseRoom,task,parserDraft,aiMemoryOutputValues(area,raw,draft));
    prepared.baseSnapshot=JSON.stringify(room);
    const candidate=prepared.candidate;
    // Manual packets may cover two outputs; automatic choices keep independent storage.
    if(area==='currentState'){
      const nextState=(candidate.slots||[]).find(slot=>slot.id==='currentState');
      candidate.slots=(room.slots||[]).map(slot=>slot.id==='currentState'?{...structuredClone(slot),content:nextState.content}:structuredClone(slot));
    }else if(area==='importantItems'){
      const saved=room.slots.find(slot=>slot.id==='currentState');
      const target=candidate.slots.find(slot=>slot.id==='currentState');
      if(saved&&target)Object.assign(target,structuredClone(saved));
      const oldItems=(room.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='item');
      const candidates=new Map((candidate.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='item').map(slot=>[String(slot.id),slot]));
      for(const old of oldItems){
        const target=candidates.get(String(old.id));
        if(!target)candidates.set(String(old.id),structuredClone(old));
        else if(old.userLocked||old.managedBy==='user'||old.editable===false)Object.assign(target,structuredClone(old));
        else {const title=target.title,content=target.content;Object.assign(target,structuredClone(old),{title,content});}
      }
      const ordered=[];
      for(const old of (room.slots||[])){
        if(old.group==='item'){ordered.push(candidates.get(String(old.id)));candidates.delete(String(old.id));}
        else ordered.push(structuredClone(old));
      }
      candidate.slots=ordered.concat([...candidates.values()]);
    }
    if(area==='currentState'){
      prepared.diffParts=(prepared.diffParts||[]).filter(part=>part.label==='현재상태.txt');
      prepared.summary=(prepared.summary||[]).filter(line=>line.startsWith('현재상태')||line.startsWith('주의'));
    }else if(area==='importantItems'){
      prepared.diffParts=(prepared.diffParts||[]).filter(part=>part.label==='중요물품.txt');
      prepared.summary=(prepared.summary||[]).filter(line=>line.startsWith('중요 물품')||line.startsWith('기존 물품'));
    }
    if((area==='currentState'||area==='importantItems')&&room.pending){
      candidate.pending=structuredClone(room.pending);
      const slotsById=new Map((candidate.slots||[]).map(slot=>[String(slot.id),slot]));
      for(const field of ['items','quickRemovedItems'])if(Array.isArray(candidate.pending[field]))candidate.pending[field]=candidate.pending[field].map(item=>{
        const id=String(item.slotId||''),slot=slotsById.get(id);
        if(!slot || (area==='currentState' ? id!=='currentState' : slot.group!=='item'))return item;
        return {...item,title:slot.title,content:String(slot.content||'').trim()};
      });
    }
    if(area==='personInfo'){
      for(const field of ['knowledgeCards','personStateRecords'])for(const old of (room[field]||[])){
        if(!(old.userLocked||old.managedBy==='user'||old.editable===false))continue;
        const list=candidate[field]||[],index=list.findIndex(card=>String(card.id)===String(old.id));
        if(index<0)list.push(structuredClone(old));else list[index]=structuredClone(old);
      }
    }
    if(area==='personInfo')candidate.personLastImportSummary=`${new Date().toLocaleString('ko-KR')} · AI 기억정리 검토 적용`;
    candidate.manualUpdateCheckpoints=structuredClone(room.manualUpdateCheckpoints||{});
    if(room.manualFullBuilds)candidate.manualFullBuilds=structuredClone(room.manualFullBuilds);else delete candidate.manualFullBuilds;
    if(area==='timeline'){
      candidate.storyTimelineLastReviewedMessageId=room.storyTimelineLastReviewedMessageId;
      candidate.storyTimelineLastReviewedAt=room.storyTimelineLastReviewedAt;
      candidate.storyTimelineUnreviewedTurnCount=room.storyTimelineUnreviewedTurnCount;
      candidate.storyTimelineCarryoverTurnKeys=structuredClone(room.storyTimelineCarryoverTurnKeys||[]);
      candidate.storyTimelinePendingReview=structuredClone(room.storyTimelinePendingReview||null);
    }
    return {...prepared,candidate};
  }
  async function aiMemoryPacketPlan(room,area,turns,options={}) {
    const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];
    const pending=aiMemoryPending(room,area,turns);
    const startIndex=options.force ? Math.max(0,Number(options.startIndex??pending.index+1)) : pending.index+1;
    if(startIndex>=turns.length)throw new Error('기준점 이후 완료된 새 RP 턴이 없습니다. 이전 범위 재검토는 수동 업데이트의 범위 지정을 사용하세요.');
    const maxEnd=Math.min(turns.length-1,startIndex+Math.max(row.interval,1)-1);
    let bundle=null,endIndex=maxEnd;
    for(;endIndex>=startIndex;endIndex--){
      try{
        const candidate=await buildManualUpdatePacket(room,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual,'standalone',{mode:'custom',startKey:turns[startIndex].key,endKey:turns[endIndex].key,loadedTurns:turns,dryRun:true,priorCheckpoint:aiMemoryIgnoredManualAnchor(room,area)});
        if(candidate.draft.packet.length>120000)continue;
        bundle=candidate;break;
      }catch(error){if(error.code!=='RPCM_FULL_SPLIT_REQUIRED'&&!/안전 표시 한도/.test(error.message))throw error;}
    }
    if(!bundle)throw new Error('한 턴 또는 지침·기존 결과가 API 안전 입력량을 넘습니다. 수동 작업자료 범위를 사용해 주세요.');
    return {startIndex,endIndex,bundle};
  }
  async function refinerRecallReference(targetText,recentMessages,roomKey,options={}) {
    const room=state.currentRoom;
    if(!room||String(room.chatId)!==String(roomKey))return null;
    const snapshot=structuredClone(room),query=[targetText,...(recentMessages||[]).slice(-8).map(row=>row.content||'')].join('\n');
    const rank=(rows,text,id,limit=8)=>{
      const docs=rows.map((row,index)=>{const raw=String(text(row));return{key:String(id(row)||index),raw,events:String(row.title||row.person||''),body:raw,index,row};});
      return scoreRelatedLogBlocks(docs,query,new Set(),snapshot).slice(0,limit).map(hit=>hit.block.row);
    };
    const log=snapshot.slots?.find(slot=>slot.id==='logSummary');
    if(log){const blocks=autoRecallVisibleLogBlocks(parseDatedLogBlocks(log.content||''),snapshot);const related=scoreRelatedLogBlocks(blocks,query,new Set(),snapshot).slice(0,5).map(hit=>hit.block);const recent=selectRecentLogBlocks(blocks,2);log.content=[...new Map([...related,...recent].map(block=>[block.key,block])).values()].map(block=>block.raw).join('\n\n')||String(log.content||'').slice(0,4000);}
    snapshot.storyTimelineCards=rank((snapshot.storyTimelineCards||[]).filter(card=>card.inject!==false),card=>`${card.title||''} ${card.content||''} ${(card.tags||[]).join(' ')}`,card=>card.cardId,6);
    snapshot.slots=(snapshot.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group!=='item'||rank([slot],row=>`${row.title||''} ${row.content||''}`,row=>row.id,1).length);
    const people=new Set([snapshot.personPlayerName,...(snapshot.personUiActors||[]).map(row=>typeof row==='string'?row:row.name),...(snapshot.slots||[]).filter(row=>row.group==='character').map(row=>row.title),...(snapshot.knowledgeCards||[]).flatMap(row=>[row.person,...(row.hiddenFrom||[]),...(row.concealments||[]).flatMap(x=>[x.holder,x.target])]),...(snapshot.personStateRecords||[]).flatMap(row=>[row.person,row.target])].filter(person=>person&&query.toLowerCase().includes(String(person).toLowerCase())));
    snapshot.knowledgeCards=(snapshot.knowledgeCards||[]).filter(row=>people.has(row.person)||(row.hiddenFrom||[]).some(person=>people.has(person))||(row.concealments||[]).some(x=>people.has(x.holder)||people.has(x.target))||rank([row],item=>`${item.fact||''} ${item.actualFact||''}`,item=>item.id,1).length);
    snapshot.personStateRecords=(snapshot.personStateRecords||[]).filter(row=>people.has(row.person)||people.has(row.target));
    let longTermText='',longTermCount=0;
    try{
      const inventory=options.includeLongTerm===false?[]:await longTermFetch(room),meta=longTermState(room);
      const related=rank(inventory,item=>`${item.title||''} ${item.summary||''}`,longTermId,6);
      const pinned=inventory.filter(item=>meta.protectedIds.includes(longTermId(item)));
      const picked=[...new Map([...pinned,...related].map(item=>[longTermId(item),item])).values()].slice(0,12);
      longTermCount=picked.length;longTermText=picked.map(item=>`[장기기억 · ${item.title||'제목 없음'}]\n${item.summary||''}`).join('\n\n').slice(0,12000);
    }catch(error){console.warn('[RP Manager] 응답교정 장기기억 회수 생략',error);}
    if(state.currentRoom!==room)throw new Error('관련 기억 조회 중 방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    return {room:snapshot,longTermText,longTermCount};
  }

  function settledAutomaticTurns(turns) {
    const refiner=window.__WISH_RP_MANAGER_REFINER__;
    if(!refiner?.canUseTurn)return turns;
    const stop=turns.findIndex((turn,index)=>(turn.reviewSources?.length?turn.reviewSources:[{id:turn.key,content:turn.assistantText}]).some(source=>!refiner.canUseTurn(String(source.id),String(source.content||''),index===turns.length-1)));
    return stop<0?turns:turns.slice(0,stop);
  }

  function sourceDraftContains(draft,messageId) {
    if(!draft)return false;
    const id=String(messageId);
    return [draft.historyPrefix,draft.historyTurns,draft.sourceTurns,draft.sourceManifest,draft.turnKeys,draft.source?.turnKeys,draft.source?.keys,draft.sourceKeys,draft.stamp?.sourceKeys].some(rows=>Array.isArray(rows)&&rows.some(row=>String(Array.isArray(row)?row[0]:row?.sourceTurnKey||row?.messageId||row?.key||row)===id)) || sourceDraftContains(draft.packetDraft,id)||sourceDraftContains(draft.stamp,id);
  }

  async function invalidateRefinerSource(messageId,previousFingerprint,nextFingerprint) {
    const room=state.currentRoom;if(!room)return;
    const affected=[];
    const mark=(draft,label)=>{if(!sourceDraftContains(draft,messageId))return false;draft.stale=true;draft.staleReason='원문 응답이 교정되었습니다. 새 원문으로 다시 검토해 주세요.';draft.sourceMutation={messageId,previousFingerprint,nextFingerprint,at:nowIso()};affected.push(label);return true;};
    for(const [area,row] of Object.entries(aiMemoryConfig(room).areas))if(mark(row.draft,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area]?.label||area))row.status='재검토 필요';
    for(const [task,job] of Object.entries(room.manualPendingJobs||{}))if(job.status!=='applied'&&mark(job.draft,MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task]?.title||task)){job.status='stale';job.stale=true;}
    for(const [key,draft] of manualUpdateRuntime.packets)if(key.startsWith(String(room.chatId))||draft.roomId===String(room.chatId))mark(draft,'수동 작업자료');
    const ltm=longTermState(room);
    for(const draft of [ltm.drafts.first,ltm.drafts.externalPacket,ltm.firstStage.autoDraft,ltm.firstStage.pendingApply]){
      if(draft&&String(draft.lastKey||draft.stamp?.lastKey||'')===String(messageId)){draft.stale=true;draft.staleReason='원문 응답이 교정되었습니다.';affected.push('장기기억');}
      else mark(draft,'장기기억');
    }
    if(mark(room.fullRebuildJob,'전체 재구축'))room.fullRebuildJob.status='stale';
    if(affected.length){await saveRoom(room);noticeUpsert(room,{type:'stale',title:'응답 교정으로 초안 재검토가 필요해요',message:[...new Set(affected)].join(' · ')+' · 새 원문으로 다시 만들어 주세요.',targetArea:'update',dedupeKey:`refiner:source:${messageId}`});}
  }

  async function aiMemoryGenerate(room,area,options={}) {
    if(!AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area])throw new Error('알 수 없는 기억 항목입니다.');
    if(aiMemoryRuntime.busy)throw new Error('다른 AI 기억 작업이 진행 중입니다.');
    if(state.currentRoom!==room)throw new Error('방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    if(state.autoSaveTimers.has(String(room.chatId))||state.saveStatus==='saving'||state.saveStatus==='error')throw new Error('편집 내용의 로컬 저장이 끝난 뒤 AI 초안을 만들어 주세요.');
    const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];
    if(row.draft&&!options.replaceDraft)throw new Error('검토 대기 중인 초안이 있습니다. 먼저 검토하거나 새로 만들기를 확인해 주세요.');
    const settings=aiFeatureSettings(loadAiSummarySettings(),area==='timeline'?'timeline':'summary');
    const provider=settings.provider,secret=readAiSecret(provider),model=settings.models?.[provider]||'';
    if(!secret){row.status='인증 필요';row.error='API 인증 정보가 없습니다. AI · API 설정에서 연결해 주세요.';row.failedEnd=String((aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.get(String(room.chatId))||{}).lastKey||'');await saveRoom(room).catch(()=>{});aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room);throw new Error(row.error);}
    aiMemoryRuntime.busy=true;aiMemoryRuntime.roomId=String(room.chatId);
    row.status='원문 확인 중';row.error='';
    const policy=Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(AI_MEMORY_AREAS).map(key=>{const config=aiMemoryConfig(room),target=config.areas[key];return [key,{autoApplyEligible:config.enabled&&target.enabled&&target.autoApply,applyRevision:target.applyRevision,globalApplyRevision:config.applyRevision,connectionStamp:JSON.stringify(aiFeatureSettings(loadAiSummarySettings(),key==='timeline'?'timeline':'summary'))}];}));
    let pairedArea='',pairedRow=null;
    try{
      const allTurns=await aiMemoryScopedTurns(room,[area,options.pairArea].filter(key=>AI_MEMORY_AREAS[key]));
      const turns=options.force?allTurns:settledAutomaticTurns(allTurns);
      if(options.force&&turns.length!==settledAutomaticTurns(allTurns).length)notify('응답교정 확인 전인 RP를 명시한 수동 실행에 사용합니다.','warn');
      if(!turns.length)throw new Error('완료된 USER+AI 턴이 없습니다.');
      const {startIndex,endIndex,bundle}=await aiMemoryPacketPlan(room,area,turns,options);
      if(options.pairArea && AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual==='currentItems' && AI_MEMORY_AREAS[options.pairArea]?.manual==='currentItems'){
        const other=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[options.pairArea];
        if(other.enabled&&!other.draft){
          try{
            const otherPlan=await aiMemoryPacketPlan(room,options.pairArea,turns);
            if(otherPlan.startIndex===startIndex&&otherPlan.endIndex===endIndex){pairedArea=options.pairArea;pairedRow=other;}
          }catch(error){console.warn('[RP매니저] 공유 호출 대신 독립 생성',error);}
        }
      }
      row.plannedStart=String(turns[startIndex].key);row.plannedEnd=String(turns[endIndex].key);
      if(pairedRow){pairedRow.plannedStart=row.plannedStart;pairedRow.plannedEnd=row.plannedEnd;pairedRow.status='API 생성 중';pairedRow.error='';}
      const task=AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual,guideMeta=manualGuideMetadata(room,task,bundle.guide);
      const historyPrefix=turns.slice(0,endIndex+1).map(turn=>[String(turn.key),String(turn.text)]);
      const targets=pairedArea?[area,pairedArea]:[area];
      const snapshots=Object.fromEntries(targets.map(key=>[key,aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(room,key)]));
      const sharedSlots=pairedArea?structuredClone((room.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.id==='currentState'||slot.group==='item')):null;
      const memoHash=simpleHash(userCanonMemoBlock(room));
      const targetLabel=pairedArea?'현재상태와 중요 물품':AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label;
      const instruction=`당신은 Wish RP Manager의 ${targetLabel} 초안을 만듭니다. 입력 [GUIDE]와 [OUTPUT_CONTRACT]를 정확히 따르세요. 제공된 REF·원문 외 사실을 만들지 마세요. 이 결과는 원문·형식·저장본 검증 후 설정에 따라 자동 반영되거나 사용자 검토를 기다립니다.${task==='currentItems'?' 출력은 JSON 객체 하나이며 키 currentState와 importantItems에 각각 두 TXT 전체본 문자열을 넣습니다. 코드펜스와 설명은 넣지 않습니다.':''}`;
      row.status='API 생성 중';aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room);
      const result=await callAiSummaryProvider(provider,settings,secret,instruction,bundle.draft.packet,16384,message=>{for(const key of targets)aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[key].status=message;},{feature:area==='timeline'?'timeline':'summary',room,allowEmptyText:false});
      if(/(?:MAX_TOKENS|LENGTH|TOKEN_LIMIT|INCOMPLETE)/i.test(String(result.finishReason||'')))throw new Error('AI 출력이 중간에 끊겼습니다. 초안을 저장하지 않았습니다.');
      if(state.currentRoom!==room)throw new Error('생성 중 방이 바뀌었습니다. 결과를 저장하지 않았습니다.');
      const combined=pairedArea?aiMemoryOutputValues(area,result.text):null;
      const sharedCallId=pairedArea?`currentItems_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`:'';
      const accepted=[],rejected=[];
      for(const key of targets){
        const target=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[key];
        const draft={...policy[key],roomId:String(room.chatId),area:key,startKey:String(turns[startIndex].key),endKey:String(turns[endIndex].key),startIndex,endIndex,turnCount:endIndex-startIndex+1,historyPrefix,historyStartKey:String(turns[0].key),endAnchorEvidence:{key:String(turns[endIndex].key),hash:aiMemoryAnchorFingerprint(turns[endIndex]),chatId:String(apiChatIdOf(room)),supersededCheckpoint:String(manualCheckpointForTask(room,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[key].manual).endMessageId||"" )},sourceHash:simpleHash(JSON.stringify(historyPrefix)),areaSnapshot:snapshots[key],memoHash,guideHash:guideMeta.hash,guideVersion:guideMeta.version,createdAt:nowIso(),packetDraft:structuredClone(bundle.draft),output:pairedArea?combined[key]:String(result.text||''),provider,model:result.model||model,usage:result.usage||null};
        if(pairedArea){draft.sharedCurrentItems=true;draft.sharedCallId=sharedCallId;draft.sharedOtherOutput=combined[key==='currentState'?'importantItems':'currentState'];draft.sharedCurrentItemsSlots=structuredClone(sharedSlots);}
        try{
          await aiMemoryAssertDraft(room,key,draft);
          aiMemoryPrepare(room,key,draft,draft.output); // Parse and validate each independent draft.
          target.draft=draft;target.status='검토 대기';target.error='';target.autoApplyError='';target.failedEnd='';target.lastGeneratedAt=nowIso();target.lastUsage=draft.usage;
          accepted.push(draft);
        }catch(error){target.status='실패';target.error=String(error.message||error);target.failedEnd=String(turns.at(-1)?.key||'');rejected.push({area:key,error});}
      }
      await saveRoom(room);aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room);
      for(const draft of accepted){const current=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[draft.area].draft;if(aiMemorySameDraft(current,draft))await aiMemoryTryAutoApply(room,draft.area,current);}
      const waiting=accepted.filter(draft=>aiMemorySameDraft(aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[draft.area].draft,draft));
      if(waiting.length)aiMemoryAnnounceDrafts(room,waiting);
      if(!accepted.length)throw rejected[0]?.error||new Error('AI 초안 검증 실패');
      return accepted.find(draft=>draft.area===area)||accepted[0];
    }catch(error){const current=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];if(!current.draft){current.status='실패';current.error=String(error.message||error);current.failedEnd=String((aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.get(String(room.chatId))||{}).lastKey||current.failedEnd||'');}if(pairedRow&&!pairedRow.draft&&pairedRow.status!=='실패'){pairedRow.status='대기';pairedRow.plannedStart='';pairedRow.plannedEnd='';}try{if(state.currentRoom===room)await saveRoom(room);}catch(_){}aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room);throw error;}
    finally{aiMemoryRuntime.busy=false;aiMemoryRuntime.roomId='';}
  }
  async function aiMemoryPoll(room,force=false) {
    const config=aiMemoryConfig(room);
    if(state.currentRoom!==room||aiMemoryRuntime.busy||document.hidden)return;
    const id=String(room.chatId),last=aiMemoryRuntime.lastPoll.get(id)||0;
    if(!force&&Date.now()-last<30000)return;
    aiMemoryRuntime.lastPoll.set(id,Date.now());
    const enabled=Object.keys(AI_MEMORY_AREAS).filter(area=>config.areas[area].enabled);
    if(!aiMemoryConfig(room).enabled||!enabled.length){aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room);return;}
    try{
      const turns=await aiMemoryPlanningTurns(room,force),counts={...(aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.get(id)||{}),lastKey:String(turns.at(-1)?.key||'')};
      if(state.currentRoom!==room||!aiMemoryConfig(room).enabled)return;
      for(const area of enabled){const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];if(!row.enabled)continue;try{counts[area]=aiMemoryPending(room,area,turns).count;}catch(error){counts[area]=null;row.error=error.message;}}
      aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.set(id,counts);
      aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room);
      if(!aiMemoryConfig(room).enabled||!enabled.length)return;
      for(const area of enabled){
        if(state.currentRoom!==room||!aiMemoryConfig(room).enabled)break;
        const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];if(!row.enabled)continue;
        if(aiMemoryManualWaiting(room,area)){row.status='수동 결과 대기 · 자동 일시정지';continue;}
        if(row.draft){const applied=await aiMemoryTryAutoApply(room,area,row.draft);if(applied)break;continue;}
        if(row.failedEnd===counts.lastKey||counts[area]==null||counts[area]<row.interval)continue;
        if(row.error){row.error='';await saveRoom(room);}
        if(state.currentRoom!==room||!aiMemoryConfig(room).enabled||!row.enabled)break;
        const peer=area==='currentState'?'importantItems':area==='importantItems'?'currentState':'';
        const peerRow=peer&&aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[peer];
        const pairArea=peerRow?.enabled&&!peerRow.draft&&peerRow.failedEnd!==counts.lastKey&&counts[peer]>=peerRow.interval?peer:'';
        await aiMemoryGenerate(room,area,pairArea?{pairArea}:{});
        break;
      }
      aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room);
    }catch(error){console.warn('[RP매니저] AI 기억정리 계획 실패',error);}
  }
  function aiMemoryChangeRows(room,area,prepared) {
    if(area==='lore')return (prepared.loreDiff||[]).filter(row=>!['unchanged','protected'].includes(row.status)).map(row=>({key:String(row.ref),label:String(row.label),kind:row.status}));
    const before=area==='importantItems'?(room.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='item')
      :area==='personInfo'?[...(room.knowledgeCards||[]),...(room.personStateRecords||[])]
      :area==='timeline'?(room.storyTimelineCards||[]):[];
    const after=area==='importantItems'?(prepared.candidate.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='item')
      :area==='personInfo'?[...(prepared.candidate.knowledgeCards||[]),...(prepared.candidate.personStateRecords||[])]
      :area==='timeline'?(prepared.candidate.storyTimelineCards||[]):[];
    const idOf=row=>String(area==='timeline'?row.cardId:row.id);
    const oldMap=new Map(before.map(row=>[idOf(row),row]));
    const afterMap=new Map(after.map(row=>[idOf(row),row]));
    const changed=after.filter(row=>JSON.stringify(oldMap.get(idOf(row))||null)!==JSON.stringify(row)).map(row=>({key:idOf(row),label:String(row.title||row.fact||`${row.person||''} → ${row.target||''}`||idOf(row)),kind:oldMap.has(idOf(row))?'수정':'추가'}));
    if(area==='timeline')changed.push(...before.filter(row=>!afterMap.has(idOf(row))).map(row=>({key:idOf(row),label:String(row.title||idOf(row)),kind:'제거'})));
    return changed;
  }
  function aiMemorySelectedPreview(room,area,draft,raw,keys,initial) {
    let source=raw;
    if(area==='lore'){
      const parsed=parseManualLoreResult(raw),selected=new Set(keys);
      const chosen=parsed.operations.filter(op=>selected.has(String(op.ref)));
      const needed=new Set(chosen.filter(op=>op.op==='card_upsert'&&String(op.packRef).startsWith('NEW_')).map(op=>String(op.packRef)));
      const filtered=JSON.stringify({version:parsed.version,operations:parsed.operations.filter(op=>selected.has(String(op.ref))||needed.has(String(op.ref)))});
      source=parsed.version==='WISH_LORE_V1'?`[[WISH_LORE_V1]]\n${filtered}\n[[/WISH_LORE_V1]]`:filtered;
    }
    const prepared=initial&&area!=='lore'?structuredClone(initial):aiMemoryPrepare(room,area,draft,source);
    if(['importantItems','personInfo','timeline'].includes(area)){
      const selected=new Set(keys),idOf=row=>String(area==='timeline'?row.cardId:row.id);
      const listFields=area==='importantItems'?['slots']:area==='personInfo'?['knowledgeCards','personStateRecords']:['storyTimelineCards'];
      for(const field of listFields){
        const oldList=area==='importantItems'?(room.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='item'):(room[field]||[]);
        const newList=area==='importantItems'?(prepared.candidate.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='item'):(prepared.candidate[field]||[]);
        const oldMap=new Map(oldList.map(row=>[idOf(row),row]));
        const kept=newList.filter(row=>selected.has(idOf(row))||JSON.stringify(oldMap.get(idOf(row))||null)===JSON.stringify(row)).map(row=>{
          if(selected.has(idOf(row)))return row;
          return structuredClone(oldMap.get(idOf(row))||row);
        });
        for(const old of oldList)if(!kept.some(row=>idOf(row)===idOf(old)) && !(area==='timeline'&&selected.has(idOf(old))))kept.push(structuredClone(old));
        if(area==='importantItems')prepared.candidate.slots=(prepared.candidate.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group!=='item').concat(kept);
        else prepared.candidate[field]=kept;
      }
      if(area==='importantItems'&&room.pending){
        prepared.candidate.pending=structuredClone(room.pending);
        const slotsById=new Map((prepared.candidate.slots||[]).map(slot=>[String(slot.id),slot]));
        for(const field of ['items','quickRemovedItems'])if(Array.isArray(prepared.candidate.pending[field]))prepared.candidate.pending[field]=prepared.candidate.pending[field].map(item=>{
          const slot=slotsById.get(String(item.slotId||''));
          return slot?.group==='item'?{...item,title:slot.title,content:String(slot.content||'').trim()}:item;
        });
      }
      const before=area==='importantItems'?(room.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='item'):area==='personInfo'?{knowledgeCards:room.knowledgeCards||[],personStateRecords:room.personStateRecords||[]}:room.storyTimelineCards||[];
      const after=area==='importantItems'?(prepared.candidate.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='item'):area==='personInfo'?{knowledgeCards:prepared.candidate.knowledgeCards||[],personStateRecords:prepared.candidate.personStateRecords||[]}:prepared.candidate.storyTimelineCards||[];
      const diff=buildAiSideBySideDiff(JSON.stringify(before,null,2),JSON.stringify(after,null,2));
      prepared.diffParts=[{label:`${AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label} · 선택 항목`,before:JSON.stringify(before,null,2),after:JSON.stringify(after,null,2),stats:diff.stats,rows:diff.rows}];
      prepared.summary=[`${selected.size}개 변경 선택`,...prepared.summary];
    }
    return prepared;
  }

  function aiMemoryRebasePreview(room,area,prepared) {
    const candidate=structuredClone(room),proposed=prepared.candidate;
    if(area==='currentState'||area==='logSummary'){
      const id=area==='currentState'?'currentState':'logSummary',target=candidate.slots.find(s=>s.id===id),source=proposed.slots.find(s=>s.id===id);
      if(!target||!source)throw new Error('초안의 저장 항목을 찾지 못했습니다.');
      target.content=source.content;
      if(area==='logSummary'){candidate.autoLogPinnedKeys=structuredClone(proposed.autoLogPinnedKeys||[]);candidate.manualLogSelectedKeys=structuredClone(proposed.manualLogSelectedKeys||[]);}
    }else if(area==='importantItems'){
      const live=new Map(candidate.slots.filter(s=>s.group==='item').map(s=>[String(s.id),s]));
      const items=proposed.slots.filter(s=>s.group==='item').map(s=>live.has(String(s.id))?{...live.get(String(s.id)),title:s.title,content:s.content}:structuredClone(s));
      candidate.slots=candidate.slots.filter(s=>s.group!=='item').concat(items);
    }else if(area==='personInfo'){
      candidate.knowledgeCards=structuredClone(proposed.knowledgeCards||[]);candidate.personStateRecords=structuredClone(proposed.personStateRecords||[]);candidate.personLastImportSummary=proposed.personLastImportSummary;
    }else if(area==='lore')candidate.lorePacks=structuredClone(proposed.lorePacks||[]);
    else if(area==='timeline')candidate.storyTimelineCards=structuredClone(proposed.storyTimelineCards||[]);
    if(candidate.pending&&['currentState','importantItems'].includes(area)){
      const slots=new Map(candidate.slots.map(s=>[String(s.id),s]));
      for(const field of ['items','quickRemovedItems'])if(Array.isArray(candidate.pending[field]))candidate.pending[field]=candidate.pending[field].map(item=>{
        const slot=slots.get(String(item.slotId||''));
        return slot&&(area==='currentState'?slot.id==='currentState':slot.group==='item')?{...item,title:slot.title,content:String(slot.content||'').trim()}:item;
      });
    }
    // Preserve the latest retention counters and unrelated work while refreshing
    // the reviewed content. The carrier is synchronized after the local commit.
    return {...prepared,candidate,baseSnapshot:JSON.stringify(room)};
  }
  function aiMemoryReadableCard(card) {
    if(!card)return '아직 저장된 항목이 없습니다.';
    if(typeof card==='string')return card;
    if(card.content!=null)return String(card.content);
    const labels={person:'인물',target:'대상',status:'인지 상태',fact:'확인된 사실',actualFact:'실제 사실',address:'호칭',tone:'말투',context:'상황',official:'공식 관계',stance:'태도·감정',conflict:'남은 갈등',evidence:'근거',dateStart:'시작 날짜',dateEnd:'종료 날짜',timelineLabel:'시간선',tags:'태그'};
    return Object.entries(labels).filter(([key])=>card[key]!=null&&String(card[key]).trim()).map(([key,label])=>`${label}: ${Array.isArray(card[key])?card[key].join(' · '):card[key]}`).join('\n')||String(card.summary||card.title||'내용 없음');
  }
  function aiMemoryReviewCardsHtml(room,area,prepared,rows) {
    const list=(r)=>area==='importantItems'?(r.slots||[]).filter(s=>s.group==='item'):area==='personInfo'?[...(r.knowledgeCards||[]),...(r.personStateRecords||[])]:area==='timeline'?r.storyTimelineCards||[]:[];
    const idOf=r=>String(area==='timeline'?r.cardId:r.id),before=new Map(list(room).map(r=>[idOf(r),r])),after=new Map(list(prepared.candidate).map(r=>[idOf(r),r]));
    const comparison=(oldText,newText)=>`<div class="rpcm-ai-review-compare"><section><h4>현재 저장된 내용</h4><pre>${esc(oldText)}</pre></section><section><h4>적용 후 내용</h4><pre>${esc(newText)}</pre></section></div>`;
    if(rows.length&&area!=='lore')return `<p class="rpcm-ai-review-note">적용할 항목을 선택하세요. 선택하지 않은 항목은 현재 내용이 유지됩니다.</p>${rows.map(row=>`<article class="rpcm-ai-review-change"><header><label><input type="checkbox" data-ai-memory-change="${esc(row.key)}" ${row.kind==='제거'?'':'checked'}><strong>${esc(row.label)}</strong></label><span class="rpcm-ai-review-kind">${esc(row.kind)}</span></header>${comparison(aiMemoryReadableCard(before.get(row.key)),after.has(row.key)?aiMemoryReadableCard(after.get(row.key)):'선택하면 이 항목을 제거합니다.')}</article>`).join('')}`;
    if(area==='lore'&&rows.length)return `<p>적용할 자료집 변경을 선택하세요.</p><div>${rows.map(row=>`<label><input type="checkbox" data-ai-memory-change="${esc(row.key)}" ${['제거','removed','deleted'].includes(row.kind)?'':'checked'}><span>${esc(row.label)}</span><small>${esc(row.kind)}</small></label>`).join('')}</div>`;
    return (prepared.diffParts||[]).map(part=>`<article class="rpcm-ai-review-change"><header><strong>${esc(part.label)}</strong></header>${comparison(String(part.before||'저장된 내용 없음'),String(part.after||'내용 없음'))}</article>`).join('');
  }
  function aiMemoryAnnounceDrafts(room,drafts) {
    if(state.currentRoom!==room||!drafts.length)return;
    document.getElementById('rpcm-ai-draft-popup')?.remove();
    const popup=document.createElement('aside');popup.id='rpcm-ai-draft-popup';popup.setAttribute('role','status');popup.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
    popup.innerHTML=`<header><strong>AI 초안이 준비됐어요</strong><button type="button" data-popup-close aria-label="알림 닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">${rpcmUiIcon('close')}</button></header><p>${esc(drafts.map(d=>AI_MEMORY_AREAS[d.area].label).join(' · '))} · 완료 RP ${Number(drafts[0].turnCount)||0}턴</p><small>변경 내용을 확인한 뒤 적용할 수 있어요.</small><div>${drafts.map(d=>`<button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-popup-review="${esc(d.area)}">${esc(AI_MEMORY_AREAS[d.area].label)} 초안 보기</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
    popup.querySelector('[data-popup-close]').onclick=()=>popup.remove();
    popup.querySelectorAll('[data-popup-review]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
      if(state.currentRoom!==room){popup.remove();return;}
      aiMemoryReviewDialog(room,button.dataset.popupReview);popup.remove();
    });
    popup.dataset.roomId=String(room.chatId);document.body.appendChild(popup);
  }
  async function aiMemorySettleReviewedSave(room,area){
    const key=String(room.chatId||'');
    if(manualUpdateRuntime.commits.has(key)||manualUpdateRuntime.commitLocks.has(key))throw new Error('다른 결과를 저장 중입니다. 완료 뒤 다시 적용해 주세요.');
    const timer=state.autoSaveTimers.get(key);
    if(timer){clearTimeout(timer);state.autoSaveTimers.delete(key);await saveRoom(room,'ai-memory-flush-editor');}
    const queue=state.roomSaveQueues?.get(key);if(queue)await queue;
    if(state.saveStatus==='error')throw new Error('로컬 저장에 실패한 편집이 있습니다. 저장을 다시 완료해 주세요.');
    let stored=await readManualStoredRoom(room);
    if(!stored)throw new Error('현재 방의 저장본을 찾지 못했습니다.');
    if(aiMemoryComparableSnapshot(aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(stored,area),area)!==aiMemoryComparableSnapshot(aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(room,area),area))throw new Error('적용할 영역의 기억이 다른 저장본에서 변경됐습니다. 새 저장본과 다시 비교해 주세요.');
    // UI normalizers and polling can add unsaved metadata at the same revision.
    // Persist those through the existing revision-protected queue before committing.
    if(JSON.stringify(stored)!==JSON.stringify(room)){
      if(Number(stored._rpcmRevision||0)!==Number(room._rpcmRevision||0)){
        const rebased=rpcmRebaseRoomOntoStored(room,stored);
        if(!rebased.ok)throw new Error(`다른 저장 작업과 충돌했습니다. ${rebased.reason}`);
        rpcmRememberRoomBaseline(stored);rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,rebased.room);
      }
      await saveRoom(room,'ai-memory-flush-metadata');stored=await readManualStoredRoom(room);
    }
    if(!stored||JSON.stringify(stored)!==JSON.stringify(room))throw new Error('저장 중 새 변경이 들어왔습니다. 변경 내용을 다시 확인해 주세요.');
    return stored;
  }

  function aiMemorySameDraft(a,b) {
    return !!a&&!!b&&a.createdAt===b.createdAt&&a.sourceHash===b.sourceHash&&a.area===b.area&&a.output===b.output;
  }
  function aiMemoryManualWaiting(room,area) {
    const task=AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual,job=room.manualPendingJobs?.[task],integrated=room.manualIntegratedJob;
    return !!(job&&!['applied','cancelled'].includes(job.status))||!!(integrated&&!['applied','cancelled'].includes(integrated.status));
  }
  function aiMemoryPolicyMatches(room,area,draft) {
    if(!room)return false;
    const config=aiMemoryConfig(room),row=config.areas[area];
    return config.enabled&&row.enabled&&row.autoApply&&!aiMemoryManualWaiting(room,area)
      &&draft.autoApplyEligible===true&&draft.applyRevision===row.applyRevision
      &&draft.globalApplyRevision===config.applyRevision
      &&draft.connectionStamp===JSON.stringify(aiFeatureSettings(loadAiSummarySettings(),area==='timeline'?'timeline':'summary'));
  }
  function aiMemoryAutoPermission(room,area,draft) {return state.currentRoom===room&&aiMemoryPolicyMatches(room,area,draft); }
  function aiMemoryEditingNow() {
    const active=document.activeElement;
    return !!document.querySelector('#rpcm-ai-memory-backdrop,#rpcm-update-dialog-backdrop,#rpcm-full-rebuild-dialog,#rpcm-compression-backdrop,#rpcm-lore-dialog-backdrop,#wish-rpcore-refiner-confirm,.rpcm-ltm-backdrop')
      ||!!(state.modal?.contains(active)&&active?.matches('textarea,input:not([type=checkbox]):not([type=button]),[contenteditable=true]'));
  }
  async function aiMemoryCommitDraft(room,area,draft,prepared,automatic=false) {
    const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area],roomKey=String(room.chatId);
    let storedSnapshot='',releaseAiCommit=null;
    const assertPermission=()=>{
      const current=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area].draft;
      if(!aiMemorySameDraft(current,draft))throw new Error('초안이 교체되거나 처리됐습니다.');
      if(current.stale)throw new Error(current.staleReason||'원문 변경으로 초안을 다시 검토해 주세요.');
      if(automatic&&(!aiMemoryAutoPermission(room,area,draft)||aiMemoryEditingNow()))throw new Error('자동 반영을 보류했습니다. 설정·작업 모드를 확인하고 초안을 검토해 주세요.');
    };
    const releaseOwnedCommit=()=>{if(releaseAiCommit){manualUpdateRuntime.commits.delete(roomKey);manualUpdateRuntime.commitLocks.delete(roomKey);releaseAiCommit();releaseAiCommit=null;}};
    try{
      assertPermission();
      await aiMemoryAssertDraft(room,area,draft);
      assertPermission();
      const latestStored=await aiMemorySettleReviewedSave(room,area);
      const lock=new Promise(resolve=>{releaseAiCommit=resolve;});manualUpdateRuntime.commits.add(roomKey);manualUpdateRuntime.commitLocks.set(roomKey,lock);
      assertPermission();
      prepared=aiMemoryRebasePreview(room,area,prepared);
      storedSnapshot=JSON.stringify(latestStored);
      const candidate=prepared.candidate,config=aiMemoryConfig(candidate),target=config.areas[area];
      config.undo.push({area,at:nowIso(),previous:JSON.parse(aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(room,area)),after:JSON.parse(aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(candidate,area)),cursor:row.cursor,baseline:row.baseline,appliedAt:row.appliedAt,lastAppliedStart:row.lastAppliedStart||'',lastAppliedEnd:row.lastAppliedEnd||''});config.undo=config.undo.filter((entry,index,all)=>all.slice(index+1).filter(next=>next.area===entry.area).length<3).slice(-18);
      if(draft.endAnchorEvidence){candidate.aiMemorySourceAnchors||={};candidate.aiMemorySourceAnchors[area]=structuredClone(draft.endAnchorEvidence);}
      target.cursor=draft.endKey;target.lastAppliedStart=draft.startKey;target.lastAppliedEnd=draft.endKey;target.appliedAt=nowIso();target.draft=null;target.status=automatic?'자동 반영 완료':'적용 완료';target.autoApplyError='';target.error='';target.plannedStart='';target.plannedEnd='';
      candidate.updatedAt=nowIso();
      await stampVerifiedResult(candidate,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual,'ai-memory',draft);
      const writeStartedAt=nowIso();
      await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=state.db.transaction(APP.storeName,'readwrite'),store=tx.objectStore(APP.storeName),request=store.get(room.chatId);let conflictReason='';request.onsuccess=()=>{try{assertPermission();}catch(error){conflictReason=error.message;tx.abort();return;}const persisted=request.result||null;if(automatic&&(!aiMemoryPolicyMatches(persisted,area,draft)||!aiMemorySameDraft(aiMemoryConfig(persisted).areas[area].draft,draft)||aiMemoryConfig(persisted).areas[area].draft?.stale)){conflictReason='다른 탭에서 자동 반영 설정 또는 초안이 변경됐습니다.';tx.abort();return;}if(JSON.stringify(room)!==prepared.baseSnapshot){conflictReason='현재 화면 데이터가 검토 뒤 다시 바뀌었습니다.';traceRoomWrite(candidate,'ai-memory:'+area,'stale-rejected',room._rpcmRevision,persisted?._rpcmRevision,'',{startedAt:writeStartedAt,before:persisted||{}});tx.abort();return;}if(JSON.stringify(persisted)!==storedSnapshot){const base=JSON.parse(storedSnapshot),sameArea=aiMemoryComparableSnapshot(aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(persisted,area),area)===aiMemoryComparableSnapshot(aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(base,area),area);if(!sameArea){conflictReason='적용하려는 기억 영역이 다른 저장 작업에서 변경됐습니다.';traceRoomWrite(candidate,'ai-memory:'+area,'stale-rejected',room._rpcmRevision,persisted?._rpcmRevision,'',{startedAt:writeStartedAt,before:persisted||{}});tx.abort();return;}const conflicts=[],merged=rpcmThreeWayNode(true,base,true,candidate,true,persisted,'',conflicts).value;if(conflicts.length){conflictReason=`동시에 수정된 항목이 있습니다: ${conflicts.slice(0,4).join(',
