')}`;traceRoomWrite(candidate,'ai-memory:'+area,'stale-rejected',room._rpcmRevision,persisted?._rpcmRevision,'',{startedAt:writeStartedAt,before:persisted||{}});tx.abort();return;}rpcmReplaceRoomObject(candidate,merged);traceRoomWrite(candidate,'ai-memory:'+area,'stale-rebased',room._rpcmRevision,persisted?._rpcmRevision,'',{startedAt:writeStartedAt,before:persisted||{},after:candidate});}candidate._rpcmRevision=Number(persisted?._rpcmRevision||0)+1;candidate.updatedAt=nowIso();store.put(candidate);};request.onerror=()=>tx.abort();tx.oncomplete=()=>{traceRoomWrite(candidate,'ai-memory:'+area,'committed',room._rpcmRevision,candidate._rpcmRevision,'',{startedAt:writeStartedAt,before:JSON.parse(storedSnapshot)||{}});resolve();};tx.onabort=()=>reject(new Error(conflictReason||'IndexedDB 저장 실패 · 어느 항목도 적용되지 않았습니다.'));tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('저장 실패'));});
      const verifiedAi=await readManualStoredRoom(room);
      if(!verifiedAi||aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(verifiedAi,area)!==aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(candidate,area)||aiMemoryConfig(verifiedAi).areas[area].cursor!==draft.endKey)
        throw new Error('AI 결과 저장 후 IndexedDB 재확인에 실패했습니다. 적용 완료로 표시하지 않았습니다.');
      const finalizedAi=await finalizeStoredResultReceipts(room,verifiedAi,[AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual],'ai-memory:'+area);
      rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,finalizedAi);state.lastSavedAt=Date.now();updateSaveStatusUi('saved');
      releaseOwnedCommit();
      notify(`${AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label} AI ${automatic?'자동 반영':'초안 적용'} 완료`,'success');
      if(room.pending){try{const result=await aiMemorySyncPending(room,area);if(result?.suspended||room.pending?.reanchorRetry)notify('로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 반영 대기','warn',6500);}catch(error){notify(`로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 반영 대기: ${error.message}`,'warn',6500);}}

      return true;
    }finally{releaseOwnedCommit();}
  }
  async function aiMemoryTryAutoApply(room,area,draft) {
    if(!aiMemoryAutoPermission(room,area,draft))return false;
    const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];
    if(row.autoApplyError||aiMemoryEditingNow())return false;
    try{
      const turns=await aiMemoryAssertSource(room,draft);
      if(settledAutomaticTurns(turns).length<=draft.endIndex)throw new Error('응답교정 확인 전인 원문은 초안 검토 후 적용해 주세요.');
      const cursor=aiMemoryEffectiveCursor(room,area,turns);
      if(cursor.index>=draft.startIndex)throw new Error('다른 작업이 이 구간을 이미 처리했습니다. 중복 자동 반영을 보류합니다.');
      const prepared=aiMemoryPrepare(room,area,draft,draft.output);
      await aiMemoryCommitDraft(room,area,draft,prepared,true);
      aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.get(String(room.chatId))&&(aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.get(String(room.chatId))[area]=Math.max(0,turns.length-draft.endIndex-1));
      aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room);return true;
    }catch(error){
      if(state.currentRoom===room&&aiMemorySameDraft(aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area].draft,draft)){
        const current=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];current.status='자동 반영 보류 · 초안 검토';current.autoApplyError=String(error.message||error);current.error='';
        await saveRoom(room).catch(()=>{});aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room);
      }
      return false;
    }
  }

  function aiMemoryReviewDialog(room,area) {
    const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area],draft=row.draft;
    if(!draft){notify('검토할 AI 초안이 없습니다.','warn');return;}
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-ai-memory-backdrop')?.remove();
    const backdrop=document.createElement('div');backdrop.id='rpcm-ai-memory-backdrop';
    backdrop.innerHTML=`<section class="rpcm-update-dialog rpcm-update-result-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="rpcm-ai-review-title"><header><div><span class="rpcm-ai-review-eyebrow">AI 기억정리 · 검토 대기</span><h2 id="rpcm-ai-review-title">${esc(AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label)} 변경 내용</h2><p>완료 RP ${draft.startIndex+1}–${draft.endIndex+1}턴 · ${draft.turnCount}턴 분석 · 아직 적용되지 않았어요</p></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-update-dialog-close" data-ai-memory-close aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">${rpcmUiIcon('close')}</button></header><div class="rpcm-update-result-body"><div class="rpcm-update-result-status" role="status">변경 내용을 불러오고 있어요…</div><div class="rpcm-ai-memory-selection"></div><details class="rpcm-ai-review-raw"><summary>초안 원문 수정</summary><p>직접 수정한 뒤 변경 내용 확인을 눌러 주세요.</p><textarea class="rpcm-ai-memory-output" spellcheck="false" aria-label="${esc(AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label)} 초안 원문"></textarea><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-ai-memory-save-draft>수정 초안 저장</button></details><details class="rpcm-ai-review-diff" hidden><summary>자세한 변경 비교</summary><div class="rpcm-update-diff"></div></details></div><footer><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-ai-memory-discard>초안 버리기</button><div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-ai-memory-check>변경 내용 확인</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-ai-memory-apply disabled>선택한 항목 적용</button></div></footer></section>`;
    const input=backdrop.querySelector('textarea');
    let companion=null;
    if(!draft.sharedCurrentItems&&['currentState','importantItems'].includes(area)){
      try{companion=aiMemoryOutputValues(area,draft.output);input.value=companion[area];}catch(_){input.value=draft.output;}
    }else input.value=draft.output;
    const editableOutput=()=>companion?JSON.stringify({...companion,[area]:input.value}):input.value;
    const status=backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-update-result-status'),diff=backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-update-diff'),diffDetails=backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-ai-review-diff'),selection=backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-ai-memory-selection');
    const check=backdrop.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-check]'),apply=backdrop.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-apply]');
    let prepared=null,initialPrepared=null,storedSnapshot='',busy=false;
    input.oninput=()=>{prepared=null;initialPrepared=null;apply.disabled=true;diffDetails.hidden=true;selection.innerHTML='';status.textContent='초안 수정됨 · 다시 검증해 주세요.';};
    const showSelectedDiff=()=>{
      const keys=[...selection.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-change]:checked')].map(input=>input.dataset.aiMemoryChange);
      if(selection.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-change]').length&&!keys.length){prepared=null;apply.disabled=true;diffDetails.hidden=true;status.textContent='적용할 변경을 하나 이상 선택해 주세요.';return;}
      selection.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-change]').forEach(input=>input.closest('.rpcm-ai-review-change')?.classList.toggle('is-excluded',!input.checked));
      apply.textContent=keys.length?`${keys.length}개 항목 적용`:'변경 내용 적용';
      prepared=aiMemorySelectedPreview(room,area,draft,editableOutput(),keys,initialPrepared);
      diff.innerHTML=`<p class="rpcm-update-diff-summary">${prepared.summary.map(esc).join(' · ')} · 선택한 변경만 적용</p>${manualUpdateDiffHtml(prepared)}`;
      diffDetails.hidden=false;apply.disabled=false;status.textContent='검증 완료 · 선택한 변경만 저장합니다. 이후 새 RP는 다음 작업에 이어서 반영됩니다.';
    };
    selection.addEventListener('change',event=>{if(!event.target.matches('[data-ai-memory-change]'))return;try{showSelectedDiff();}catch(error){prepared=null;apply.disabled=true;status.textContent=`선택 검증 실패: ${error.message}`;}});
    check.onclick=async()=>{check.disabled=true;apply.disabled=true;status.textContent='원문·지침·저장본 재검증 중…';try{
      await aiMemoryAssertDraft(room,area,draft);
      const initial=aiMemoryPrepare(room,area,draft,editableOutput());initialPrepared=initial;
      const stored=await aiMemorySettleReviewedSave(room,area);
      if(!stored||aiMemoryComparableSnapshot(aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(stored,area),area)!==aiMemoryComparableSnapshot(aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(room,area),area))throw new Error('현재 화면과 저장된 기억이 다릅니다. 저장이 완료된 뒤 다시 확인해 주세요.');
      storedSnapshot=JSON.stringify(stored);
      const rows=aiMemoryChangeRows(room,area,initial);
      selection.innerHTML=aiMemoryReviewCardsHtml(room,area,initial,rows);
      showSelectedDiff();
    }catch(error){prepared=null;status.textContent=`적용 불가: ${error.message}`;}finally{check.disabled=false;}};
    apply.onclick=async()=>{if(!prepared||busy)return;if(!confirm(`${AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label} AI 초안을 저장할까요?\n수동 체크포인트는 변경하지 않습니다.`))return;busy=true;apply.disabled=true;status.textContent='원문·저장본 최종 확인 및 저장 중…';const roomKey=String(room.chatId);let releaseAiCommit=null;const releaseOwnedCommit=()=>{if(releaseAiCommit){manualUpdateRuntime.commits.delete(roomKey);manualUpdateRuntime.commitLocks.delete(roomKey);releaseAiCommit();releaseAiCommit=null;}};try{
      await aiMemoryCommitDraft(room,area,draft,prepared,false);
      status.textContent='저장 완료 · AI 적용 기준점이 이 항목에서만 이동했습니다.';
      backdrop.remove();renderModalIfOpen();
    }catch(error){status.textContent=`저장 실패: ${error.message}`;apply.disabled=false;}finally{releaseOwnedCommit();busy=false;}};
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-save-draft]').onclick=async()=>{draft.output=editableOutput();await saveRoom(room);prepared=null;apply.disabled=true;status.textContent='수정한 초안을 저장했습니다. 적용 전에 다시 검증해 주세요.';};
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-discard]').onclick=async()=>{if(busy||!confirm('미적용 AI 초안을 버릴까요? 저장된 기억은 그대로 유지됩니다.'))return;row.draft=null;row.status='대기';row.plannedStart='';row.plannedEnd='';await saveRoom(room);backdrop.remove();renderModalIfOpen();};
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-close]').onclick=()=>{if(!busy)backdrop.remove();};
    backdrop.onkeydown=event=>{if(event.key==='Escape'&&!busy)backdrop.remove();};
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);backdrop.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-close]').focus();void check.onclick();
  }


  async function aiMemorySyncPending(room,area) {
    if(!room.pending)return;
    if(area==='personInfo')return rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room);
    if(area==='timeline')return rebuildPendingStoryTimelineItem(room,'ai-memory-timeline');
    if(area==='logSummary')return rebuildPendingLogItems(room,'ai-memory-log');
    if(area==='lore'){
      const previous=clonePendingItems(room.pending.items);
      refreshPendingLoreItems(room);
      try{return await syncPendingCarrier(room,'ai-memory-lore');}
      catch(error){room.pending.items=previous;savePendingBackup(room.chatId,room.pending);await saveRoom(room).catch(()=>{});throw error;}
    }
    const previous=structuredClone(room.pending);
    const slots=new Map((room.slots||[]).map(slot=>[String(slot.id),slot]));
    for(const field of ['items','quickRemovedItems'])if(Array.isArray(room.pending[field]))room.pending[field]=room.pending[field].map(item=>{
      const slot=slots.get(String(item.slotId||''));
      if(!slot || (area==='currentState'?slot.id!=='currentState':slot.group!=='item'))return item;
      return {...item,title:slot.title,content:String(slot.content||'').trim()};
    });
    savePendingBackup(room.chatId,room.pending);
    try{return await syncPendingCarrier(room,`ai-memory-${area}`);}
    catch(error){room.pending=previous;savePendingBackup(room.chatId,room.pending);await saveRoom(room).catch(()=>{});throw error;}
  }

  async function aiMemoryUndo(room,area) {
    const config=aiMemoryConfig(room),index=config.undo.map(entry=>entry.area).lastIndexOf(area);
    if(index<0)throw new Error('이 항목에 되돌릴 AI 적용 기록이 없습니다.');
    const entry=config.undo[index];
    if(aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(room,area)!==JSON.stringify(entry.after))throw new Error('AI apply was followed by a manual edit. Undo is blocked to protect it.');
    if(!confirm(`${AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label}의 직전 AI 적용 내용과 AI 기준점을 되돌릴까요? 현재 저장본의 이후 수동 편집은 덮어쓸 수 있습니다.`))return;
    const stored=await readManualStoredRoom(room);
    if(JSON.stringify(stored)!==JSON.stringify(room))throw new Error('저장본이 바뀌었습니다. 다시 열어 확인해 주세요.');
    const candidate=structuredClone(room),slots=candidate.slots||[],previous=structuredClone(entry.previous);
    if(area==='currentState'){const i=slots.findIndex(slot=>slot.id==='currentState');if(i>=0&&previous)slots[i]=previous;}
    else if(area==='importantItems')candidate.slots=slots.filter(slot=>slot.group!=='item').concat(previous||[]);
    else if(area==='logSummary'){
      const i=slots.findIndex(slot=>slot.id==='logSummary');if(i>=0&&previous?.slot)slots[i]=previous.slot;
      candidate.autoLogPinnedKeys=previous?.pinned||[];candidate.manualLogSelectedKeys=previous?.selected||[];
    }else if(area==='personInfo'){candidate.knowledgeCards=previous?.knowledge||[];candidate.personStateRecords=previous?.records||[];}
    else if(area==='lore')candidate.lorePacks=previous||[];
    else if(area==='timeline'){candidate.storyTimelineCards=previous?.cards||[];candidate.storyTimelineLastReviewedMessageId=previous?.reviewed||'';}
    const target=aiMemoryConfig(candidate).areas[area];target.cursor=entry.cursor;target.baseline=entry.baseline;target.appliedAt=entry.appliedAt;target.lastAppliedStart=entry.lastAppliedStart;target.lastAppliedEnd=entry.lastAppliedEnd;target.status='되돌림';target.draft=null;
    candidate.aiMemoryAutomation.undo.splice(index,1);candidate.updatedAt=nowIso();
    await stampVerifiedResult(candidate,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual,'ai-memory-undo');
    const writeStartedAt=nowIso();
    await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=state.db.transaction(APP.storeName,'readwrite'),store=tx.objectStore(APP.storeName),request=store.get(room.chatId);request.onsuccess=()=>{if(JSON.stringify(request.result||null)!==JSON.stringify(stored)){traceRoomWrite(candidate,'ai-memory-undo:'+area,'stale-rejected',room._rpcmRevision,request.result?._rpcmRevision,'',{startedAt:writeStartedAt,before:request.result||{}});tx.abort();return;}candidate._rpcmRevision=Number(request.result?._rpcmRevision||0)+1;store.put(candidate);};request.onerror=()=>tx.abort();tx.oncomplete=()=>{traceRoomWrite(candidate,'ai-memory-undo:'+area,'committed',room._rpcmRevision,candidate._rpcmRevision,'',{startedAt:writeStartedAt,before:stored});resolve();};tx.onabort=()=>reject(new Error('저장본 변경 또는 IndexedDB 실패 · 되돌리지 않았습니다.'));});
    const verifiedUndo=await readManualStoredRoom(room);
    if(!verifiedUndo||aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(verifiedUndo,area)!==aiMemoryAreaSnapshot(candidate,area))throw new Error('되돌린 결과를 IndexedDB에서 확인하지 못했습니다.');
    const finalizedUndo=await finalizeStoredResultReceipts(room,verifiedUndo,[AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual],'ai-memory-undo:'+area);
    rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,finalizedUndo);if(room.pending){try{const result=await aiMemorySyncPending(room,area);if(result?.suspended||room.pending?.reanchorRetry)notify('로컬 되돌리기 완료 · 현재 주입 반영 대기','warn');}catch(error){notify(`로컬 되돌리기 완료 · 현재 주입 반영 대기: ${error.message}`,'warn');}}
  }

  function bindAiMemoryPreferences(overlay,room) {
    const config=aiMemoryConfig(room);
    overlay.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-global]')?.addEventListener('change',async event=>{const config=aiMemoryConfig(room);config.enabled=event.target.checked;config.applyRevision++;await saveRoom(room);renderModalIfOpen();if(config.enabled)void aiMemoryPoll(room,true);});
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-enable]').forEach(input=>input.addEventListener('change',()=>aiMemorySetEnabled(room,input.dataset.aiMemoryEnable,input.checked,input)));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-auto-apply]').forEach(input=>input.addEventListener('change',async()=>{
      const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[input.dataset.aiMemoryAutoApply],previous=row.autoApply;
      row.autoApply=input.checked;row.applyRevision++;
      try{await saveRoom(room);aiMemoryRefreshStatusUi(room);notify(row.autoApply?'이후 생성되는 유효한 AI 결과는 자동 반영합니다. 기존 초안은 검토 후 적용해 주세요.':'수동 검토 모드 · 기존 초안과 기준점은 유지합니다.','success');}
      catch(error){row.autoApply=previous;input.checked=previous;notify(error.message,'error');}
    }));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-interval]').forEach(input=>input.addEventListener('change',async()=>{const area=input.dataset.aiMemoryInterval,row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area],value=Number(input.value);if(!Number.isInteger(value)||value<1||value>100){input.value=String(row.interval);notify('주기는 1~100 완료 턴으로 지정해 주세요.','warn');return;}row.interval=value;await saveRoom(room);renderModalIfOpen();}));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-run]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',async()=>{button.disabled=true;try{const area=button.dataset.aiMemoryRun,row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];if(row.draft&&!confirm('기존 미적용 초안을 버리고 새로 생성할까요?'))return;await aiMemoryGenerate(room,area,{force:true,replaceDraft:true});}catch(error){notify(`AI 기억정리 실패: ${error.message}`,'error',6500);}finally{if(button.isConnected)button.disabled=false;}}));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-review]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>aiMemoryReviewDialog(room,button.dataset.aiMemoryReview)));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-undo]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{await aiMemoryUndo(room,button.dataset.aiMemoryUndo);renderModalIfOpen();notify('AI 적용 내용과 해당 항목 기준점을 함께 되돌렸습니다.','success');}catch(error){notify(`되돌리기 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}}));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-now]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
      const area=button.dataset.aiMemoryNow,row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];
      if(row.draft&&!confirm('검토 대기 중인 초안을 버리고 지금부터 새 RP만 셀까요?'))return;
      button.disabled=true;
      try{await aiMemoryStartFromNow(room,area);renderModalIfOpen();notify(`${AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label}은 지금부터 완료 RP를 셉니다.`,'success');}
      catch(error){button.disabled=false;notify(`시작점 저장 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}
    }));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-memory-baseline]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>openAiMemoryBaselineDialog(room,button.dataset.aiMemoryBaseline).catch(error=>notify(`시작점 확인 실패: ${error.message}`,'error'))));
  }

  const RPCM_RESPONSE_REFINER_SETTINGS_KEY = 'wish-rp-core-response-refiner-settings-v3';
  function rpcmResponseRefinerDisplayPrefs() {
    try {
      const raw=GM_getValue(RPCM_RESPONSE_REFINER_SETTINGS_KEY,'');
      const saved=raw?JSON.parse(raw):{};
      return {showButton:saved.showButton!==false,showBadge:saved.showBadge!==false};
    } catch (_) { return {showButton:true,showBadge:true}; }
  }
  function rpcmSetResponseRefinerDisplayPref(field,value) {
    if(!['showButton','showBadge'].includes(field))return false;
    try {
      const raw=GM_getValue(RPCM_RESPONSE_REFINER_SETTINGS_KEY,'');
      const saved=raw?JSON.parse(raw):{};
      saved[field]=!!value;
      GM_setValue(RPCM_RESPONSE_REFINER_SETTINGS_KEY,JSON.stringify(saved));
      window.__WISH_RP_MANAGER_REFINER__?.setDisplayPreference?.(field,!!value);
      return true;
    } catch (_) { return false; }
  }

  function renderRpcmPreferences(room) {
    const ai=aiFeatureSettings(loadAiSummarySettings(),'summary'),provider=ai.provider,model=ai.models?.[provider]||'';
    const secret=!!readAiSecret(provider);
    const refinerDisplay=rpcmResponseRefinerDisplayPrefs();
    const guideRows=['currentItems','logSummary','personInfo','timeline','lore'].map(task=>{const meta=manualGuideMetadata(room,task);return `<div class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>${esc(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task].title)}<small>${esc(meta.version)} · ${esc(meta.source)}</small></span><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-rpcm-pref-guide="${task}">지침</button></div>`;}).join('');
    return `<div class="rpcm-pref-grid rpcm-settings-grid">
      <article class="rpcm-pref-card"><header><h3>AI 연결 ${rpcmHelpButton('ai')}</h3><span class="rpcm-pref-scope">이 기기</span></header><p>${secret?'인증 저장됨':'인증 필요'} · ${esc(provider)} · ${esc(model)}</p><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-rpcm-pref-action="api">API · 모델 설정</button></article>
      <article class="rpcm-pref-card"><header><h3>기억 회수·주입 ${rpcmHelpButton('recall')}</h3><span class="rpcm-pref-scope">현재 방</span></header>
        <label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>최근·관련 날짜로그 자동 선택<small>직접 선택·고정·제외는 기억 화면에서 관리</small></span><input type="checkbox" data-rpcm-pref-field="autoLogRecallEnabled" ${room.autoLogRecallEnabled?'checked':''}></label>
        <label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>관련도 기준<small>최근 연속성 로그에는 적용되지 않음</small></span><select data-rpcm-pref-field="autoLogRelevance"><option value="strict" ${room.autoLogRelevance==='strict'?'selected':''}>엄격</option><option value="balanced" ${room.autoLogRelevance==='balanced'?'selected':''}>균형</option><option value="broad" ${room.autoLogRelevance==='broad'?'selected':''}>넓게</option></select></label>
        <label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>AI 맥락 검토 ${rpcmHelpButton('context')}<small>관련 날짜로그 후보만 API로 재검토</small></span><input type="checkbox" data-rpcm-pref-field="aiContextLogRerankEnabled" ${room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled?'checked':''}></label></article>
      ${semanticUiHtml(room)}
      <article class="rpcm-pref-card"><header><h3>화면·모니터</h3><span class="rpcm-pref-scope">이 기기</span></header><p>상단 WISH 버튼을 짧게 누르면 빠른 관리, 길게 누르면 전체 Manager가 열립니다. 작은 초록색 표시가 있으면 주입이 켜진 상태입니다.</p>
        <label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>응답교정 바로가기 표시<small>크랙 채팅 상단의 🪽 응답교정 버튼을 표시합니다. 꺼도 Manager의 AI 화면에서 응답교정 설정을 열 수 있습니다.</small></span><input type="checkbox" data-rpcm-refiner-ui-field="showButton" ${refinerDisplay.showButton?'checked':''}></label>
        <label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>응답교정 검토 알림 표시<small>검토 중·검토 완료 상태를 채팅 화면 상단에 잠시 표시합니다.</small></span><input type="checkbox" data-rpcm-refiner-ui-field="showBadge" ${refinerDisplay.showBadge?'checked':''}></label>
        <button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-rpcm-pref-action="quick">Quick Panel 열기</button></article>
      <article class="rpcm-pref-card"><header><h3>AI 작업</h3><span class="rpcm-pref-scope">이 기기 · 현재 방</span></header><p>AI 요약은 현재상태·날짜로그의 수동 실행 도구입니다. 결과 확인 후 선택 적용합니다.</p><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-rpcm-pref-action="summary">AI 요약 열기</button></article>
      <article class="rpcm-pref-card"><header><h3>지침·백업 ${rpcmHelpButton('guide')}</h3><span class="rpcm-pref-scope">지침별 · 현재 방</span></header>${guideRows}<div class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>전체 재구축 Master<small>기본 v1.0.3 또는 사용자 수정본</small></span><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-rpcm-pref-action="rebuild-guide">지침</button></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-rpcm-pref-action="backup">백업·복원 열기</button></article>
    </div>`;
  }
  function bindRpcmPreferences(overlay,room) {
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-rpcm-pref-field]').forEach(input=>input.addEventListener('change',async()=>{
      const field=input.dataset.rpcmPrefField, old=room[field], value=input.type==='checkbox'?input.checked:input.value;
      const main=field==='autoLogRecallEnabled'?overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-auto-log'):field==='autoLogRelevance'?overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-auto-log-relevance'):overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-ai-context-toggle');
      if(main){main.value=input.type==='checkbox'?main.value:value;if(input.type==='checkbox')main.checked=value;main.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}
      else {room[field]=value;try{await saveRoom(room);}catch(error){room[field]=old;notify(`설정 저장 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}}
    }));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-rpcm-refiner-ui-field]').forEach(input=>input.addEventListener('change',()=>{
      const field=input.dataset.rpcmRefinerUiField,value=input.checked;
      if(!rpcmSetResponseRefinerDisplayPref(field,value)){input.checked=!value;notify('응답교정 화면 설정을 저장하지 못했습니다.','error');return;}
      notify(field==='showButton'?(value?'응답교정 바로가기를 표시합니다.':'응답교정 바로가기를 숨겼습니다.'):(value?'응답교정 검토 알림을 표시합니다.':'응답교정 검토 알림을 숨겼습니다.'),'success',2200);
    }));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-rpcm-pref-guide]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>{const task=button.dataset.rpcmPrefGuide;const guide=manualGuideMetadata(room,task);openManualUpdateTextDialog(`${MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task].title} · 지침`,`${guide.source} · ${guide.version} · ${guide.hash}`,manualGuideOnlyText(room,task),'내용 복사');}));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-rpcm-pref-action]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const action=button.dataset.rpcmPrefAction;
      if(action==='api'){showWorkView(overlay,'ai',true);overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-ai-inline-settings')?.click();}
      else if(action==='quick')openQuickInjectionPanel().catch(error=>notify(error.message,'error'));
      else if(action==='summary')overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-ai-summary')?.click();
      else if(action==='backup')overlay.querySelector('[data-jump="rpcm-section-tools"]')?.click();
      else if(action==='rebuild-guide')overlay.querySelector('[data-rebuild-guide]')?.click();
    }));
  }

  function replaceManagerContents(overlay, markup) {
    const template=document.createElement('template');template.innerHTML=markup;
    const wrap=overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-modal-wrap'),nextWrap=template.content.querySelector('#rpcm-modal-wrap');
    const panel=wrap?.querySelector('#rpcm-modal'),nextPanel=nextWrap?.querySelector('#rpcm-modal');
    if(wrap&&panel&&nextWrap&&nextPanel){
      panel.dataset.rpcmRefreshed = 'true';
      panel.replaceChildren(...nextPanel.childNodes);
    }else overlay.replaceChildren(template.content);
  }

  function renderModal() {
    rpcmNewBadgeSchedule();
    const overlay = state.modal;
    const room = state.currentRoom;
    if (!overlay || !room) return;
    rpcmBindEditingGuards();
    if (overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-body') && overlay.contains(document.activeElement) && rpcmTextEditor(document.activeElement)) {
      overlay._rpcmDeferredEditorRender = true;
      return;
    }
    if (rpcmHasActiveComposition()) { overlay._rpcmDeferredEditorRender = true; return; }
    overlay._rpcmDeferredEditorRender = false;
    noticeReconcile(room);
    ensureUserCanonMemoStyles();
    addLongTermStyles();
    const ui2TransientState = captureUi2TransientState(overlay, room);
    // 전체 재렌더가 일어나면 기존 focus card도 사라지므로 배경 shade도 같이 정리합니다.
    document.querySelector('.rpcm-focus-shade')?.remove();

    // 비동기 설정 변경 후 전체 재렌더가 필요해도 사용자가 보고 있던 위치를 유지합니다.
    const previousBody = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-body');
    const previousScrollTop = previousBody ? previousBody.scrollTop : null;
    const previousContentScrollTop = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-work-content')?.scrollTop || 0;
    const previousTimelineMonths = [...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-inline-story-month[data-month-key]')];
    if (previousTimelineMonths.length) state.timelineMonthOpenByRoom.set(String(room.chatId), new Map(previousTimelineMonths.map(node => [String(node.dataset.monthKey), node.open])));
    const previousSummaryOpen = !!overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-summary[open]');
    const previousItemToolsOpen = !!overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-section-more[open]');
    const previousItemBulkOpen = !!overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-item-bulkbar[open]');
    const hadPreviousRender = !!previousBody;
    const previouslyOpenKnowledge = new Set([...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-knowledge-card[open][data-knowledge-id]')].map(node => String(node.dataset.knowledgeId || '')));
    const hadPersonProfiles = !!overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-profile');
    const previouslyOpenProfiles = new Set([...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-profile[open][data-person-name]')].map(node => String(node.dataset.personName || '')));
    const previousPersonImportText = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-person-import-text')?.value || state.personImportDraft || '';
    const previousPersonImportOpen = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-person-import')?.hidden === false || state.personImportOpen === true;
    const previouslyRawOpen = new Set([...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-slot[data-slot-id] .rpcm-edit:not([hidden])')].map(node => String(node.closest('.rpcm-slot')?.dataset.slotId || '')));

      button.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
      if (active) button.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page'); else button.removeAttribute('aria-current');
    });
    const currentStateWrap = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-current-state-slot');
    const logSummaryWrap = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-log-summary-slot');
    const charWrap = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-character-slots');
    const itemWrap = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-item-slots');
    const extraWrap = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-extra-slots');
    const memoryTabs = [...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-memory-tabs [data-memory-pane]')];
    const activateMemoryPane = key => {
      const pane = ['state', 'logs'].includes(key) ? key : 'state';
      state.memoryPane = pane;
      memoryTabs.forEach(button => {
        const selected = button.dataset.memoryPane === pane;
        button.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(selected));
        button.tabIndex = selected ? 0 : -1;
      });
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-memory-pane').forEach(node => { node.hidden = node.dataset.memoryContent !== pane; });
      if (['memory','logs'].includes(overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-workspace')?.dataset.view)) showWorkView(overlay, pane === 'logs' ? 'logs' : 'memory');
    };
    memoryTabs.forEach((button, index) => {
      button.onclick = () => activateMemoryPane(button.dataset.memoryPane);
      button.onkeydown = event => {
        const offset = event.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : event.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -1 : 0;
        if (!offset) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        const next = memoryTabs[(index + offset + memoryTabs.length) % memoryTabs.length];
        activateMemoryPane(next.dataset.memoryPane); next.focus();
      };
    });
    activateMemoryPane(state.memoryPane);

    overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-auto-inline-toggle').forEach(btn => {
      btn.onclick = () => {
        const row = btn.closest('.rpcm-auto-active-row');
        const content = row?.querySelector('.rpcm-auto-inline-content');
        if (!content) return;
        content.hidden = !content.hidden;
        btn.textContent = content.hidden ? '▾' : '▴';
        btn.title = content.hidden ? '로그 내용 펼치기' : '로그 내용 접기';
        btn.setAttribute('aria-label', btn.title);
      };
    });

    overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-auto-remove').forEach(btn => {
      btn.onclick = async () => {
        const row = btn.closest('.rpcm-auto-active-row');
        const key = String(row?.dataset.pendingKey || '');
        if (!key) return;
        btn.disabled = true;
        try {
          await removePendingRecallItem(room, key, String(row?.dataset.sourceKey || ''), String(row?.dataset.autoType || ''));
          notify(room.pending ? '이 항목을 현재 주입에서 제외했습니다.' : '이 로그를 다음 주입 목록에서 제외했습니다.', 'success', 3200);
          renderModalIfOpen();
        } catch (error) {
          btn.disabled = false;
          notify(`로그 빼기 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error', 6000);
        }
      };
    });

    overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-auto-reroll').forEach(btn => {
      btn.onclick = async () => {
        const key = String(btn.closest('.rpcm-auto-active-row')?.dataset.pendingKey || '');
        if (!key) return;
        btn.disabled = true;
        try {
          const selectedKey = await openRelatedLogPickerDialog(room, { replaceIdentity:key });
          if (!selectedKey) { btn.disabled = false; return; }
          await applyManualRelatedLog(room, selectedKey, key);
          notify('확인한 날짜로그로 교체했습니다. ‘직접 주입’으로 유지됩니다.', 'success', 4200);
          renderModalIfOpen();
        } catch (error) {
          btn.disabled = false;
          notify(`관련로그 교체 실패: ${error.message}`, 'warn', 6000);
        }
      };
    });

    const relatedAddBtn = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-related-add');
    if (relatedAddBtn) relatedAddBtn.onclick = async () => {
      relatedAddBtn.disabled = true;
      try {
        const selectedKey = await openRelatedLogPickerDialog(room);
        if (!selectedKey) { relatedAddBtn.disabled = false; return; }
        await applyManualRelatedLog(room, selectedKey);
        notify('관련로그를 직접 추가했습니다. AI 맥락 재검토 후에도 유지됩니다.', 'success', 4200);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      } catch (error) {
        relatedAddBtn.disabled = false;
        notify(`관련로그 추가 실패: ${error.message}`, 'warn', 6000);
      }
    };

    const aiContextButtons = [...overlay.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-ai-context-run,#rpcm-ai-context-refresh')];
    for (const button of aiContextButtons) button.onclick = async event => {
      event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
      if (room._rpcmContextReviewBusy) return;
      Object.defineProperty(room,'_rpcmContextReviewBusy',{value:true,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});
      const labels = aiContextButtons.map(node => node.textContent);
      aiContextButtons.forEach(node => { node.disabled=true; node.textContent='검토 중…'; });
      try {
        readModalIntoRoom();
        const report = await refreshAiContextReviewNow(room);
        if (report?.state === 'success') {
          notify(`AI 맥락 검토 완료 · API 검토 ${Number(report.inspectedCount||0)}개 · 과거로그 ${Number(report.selectedCount||0)}개 선정${room.pending?' · 현재 주입 확인':' · 다음 주입 후보'}${report.note?' · '+report.note:''}`, 'success', 6000);
        } else if (report?.state === 'skipped') {
          notify(`AI 맥락 검토 대기 · ${report.note||'설정을 확인해 주세요.'}`, 'warn', 6500);
        } else if (report?.state === 'fallback') {
          notify(`AI 맥락 검토 실패 · 검색 결과 유지 · ${report.note||report.failureDetail||'상세 기록을 확인하세요.'}`, 'warn', 6500);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        notify(`AI 맥락 검토 실패: ${error.message}`, 'warn', 6500);
      } finally {
        delete room._rpcmContextReviewBusy;
        aiContextButtons.forEach((node,index) => { node.disabled=false; node.textContent=labels[index]; });
        renderModalIfOpen();
      }
    };

    const resolveDupBtn = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-resolve-duplicate-logs');
    if (resolveDupBtn) resolveDupBtn.onclick = async () => {
      readModalIntoRoom();
      const changed = await openDuplicateLogResolverDialog(room);
      if (!changed) return;
      let localSaved = false;
      try {
        const logSlot = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === 'logSummary');
        const cleanedContent = String(logSlot?.content || '');
        const remainingDuplicates = duplicateLogDateGroups(room);
        if (remainingDuplicates.length) {
          throw new Error(`정리 후에도 중복 날짜가 남아 있습니다: ${remainingDuplicates.map(g => `${g.label} (${g.blocks.length}개)`).join(', ')}`);
        }

        // 정리 창은 room 데이터를 직접 바꾸므로, 뒤쪽의 기존 textarea가 낡은 원문을
        // 다시 저장하지 않게 화면 값도 즉시 같은 내용으로 맞춥니다.
        const logTextarea = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-slot[data-slot-id="logSummary"] .rpcm-textarea');
        if (logTextarea) logTextarea.value = cleanedContent;
        const queuedSave = state.autoSaveTimers.get(String(room.chatId || ''));
        if (queuedSave) clearTimeout(queuedSave);
        state.autoSaveTimers.delete(String(room.chatId || ''));

        // 서버 carrier 갱신이 실패하더라도 정리한 로그 원문은 잃지 않도록 로컬에 먼저 저장합니다.
        await saveRoom(room);
        localSaved = true;
        renderModalIfOpen();

        if (room.pending) await rebuildPendingLogItems(room, 'duplicate-log-resolve');
        await saveRoom(room);
        notify('중복 날짜 로그 정리를 반영했습니다.', 'success', 4200);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      } catch (e) {
        notify(localSaved
          ? `중복 로그 원문은 저장했지만 현재 주입 갱신에 실패했습니다: ${e.message}`
          : `중복 로그 정리 반영 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 7200);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      }
    };

    const resolveDuplicateItemsBtn = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-resolve-duplicate-items');
    if (resolveDuplicateItemsBtn) resolveDuplicateItemsBtn.onclick = async () => {
      if (room.pending) { notify('중복 물품 정리는 현재 주입을 해제한 뒤 진행해 주세요.', 'warn', 4800); return; }
      readModalIntoRoom();
      const decisions = await openImportantItemDuplicateResolverDialog(room);
      if (!decisions) return;
      const removed = applyImportantItemDuplicateDecisions(room, decisions);
      await saveRoom(room);
      notify(removed ? `중복 물품을 정리했습니다. ${removed}개 제거 · 선택한 항목 유지` : '중복 물품을 전체 유지했습니다.', 'success', 4300);
      renderModalIfOpen();
    };

    const normalizeDatesWarningBtn = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-normalize-log-dates');
    if (normalizeDatesWarningBtn) normalizeDatesWarningBtn.onclick = async () => {
      readModalIntoRoom();
      const changed = await openLogDateNormalizerDialog(room);
      if (!changed) return;
      try {
        if (room.pending) await rebuildPendingLogItems(room, 'log-date-normalize');
        await saveRoom(room);
        notify('로그 제목의 날짜/연도 수정을 반영했습니다.', 'success', 4200);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      } catch (e) { notify(`날짜 수정 반영 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6500); }
    };

    // Moving a card between folders only changes that card and its two folder
    // containers. Rebuilding the whole Manager here caused the visible flash.
    function moveSlotFolderInPlace(card, slot) {
      const isItem=slot.group==='item';
      const wrap=isItem?itemWrap:extraWrap;
      const folderClass=isItem?'rpcm-item-folder':'rpcm-extra-folder';
      const bodyClass=isItem?'rpcm-item-folder-body':'rpcm-extra-folder-body';
      const dataKey=isItem?'itemFolder':'extraCategory';
      const name=String(isItem?slot.itemFolder:slot.extraCategory||'').trim()||'미분류';
      const dataName=isItem&&name==='미분류'&&!String(slot.itemFolder||'').trim()?'':name;
      const old=card.closest('.'+folderClass);
      let target=[...wrap.querySelectorAll('.'+folderClass)].find(node=>node.dataset[dataKey]===dataName);
      if(!target){
        target=document.createElement('details');
        target.className=folderClass;
        target.dataset[dataKey]=dataName;
        target.open=true;
        target.innerHTML=`<summary><strong>${!dataName?'🗂️':'📁'} ${esc(name)}</strong><span>0개</span></summary><div class="${bodyClass}"></div>`;
        const drop=wrap.querySelector('.rpcm-unclassified-drop');
        if(drop)wrap.insertBefore(target,drop);else wrap.appendChild(target);
      }
      target.querySelector('.'+bodyClass).appendChild(card);
      for(const node of [old,target]){
        if(!node)continue;
        const count=node.querySelectorAll(':scope > .'+bodyClass+' > .rpcm-slot').length;
        if(!count){node.remove();continue;}
        const label=node.querySelector(':scope > summary > span');
        if(label)label.textContent=`${count}개`;
      }
      if(isItem){
        const actors=personCoreViewModel(room).actors;
        const actorFor=folder=>actors.find(actor=>actor.name===folder)||actors.find(actor=>(actor.aliases||[]).includes(folder));
        const rank=folder=>!folder?-1:actorFor(folder)?actors.indexOf(actorFor(folder)):actors.length;
        [...wrap.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-item-folder')].sort((a,b)=>rank(a.dataset.itemFolder)-rank(b.dataset.itemFolder)).forEach(node=>wrap.insertBefore(node,wrap.querySelector('.rpcm-unclassified-drop')));
        wrap.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-item-folder').forEach(node=>{const folder=node.dataset.itemFolder;node.hidden=!(state.itemFolderFilter==='all'||(state.itemFolderFilter==='unclassified'&&!folder)||(state.itemFolderFilter==='other'&&!!folder&&!actorFor(folder))||(state.itemFolderFilter.startsWith('person:')&&actorFor(folder)?.name===state.itemFolderFilter.slice(7)));});
        const counts={all:room.slots.filter(row=>row.group==='item').length,unclassified:room.slots.filter(row=>row.group==='item'&&!String(row.itemFolder||'').trim()).length,other:room.slots.filter(row=>row.group==='item'&&String(row.itemFolder||'').trim()&&!actorFor(String(row.itemFolder||'').trim())).length};
        wrap.previousElementSibling?.querySelectorAll('[data-item-filter]').forEach(tab=>{const key=tab.dataset.itemFilter;const n=key.startsWith('person:')?room.slots.filter(row=>row.group==='item'&&actorFor(String(row.itemFolder||'').trim())?.name===key.slice(7)).length:counts[key]||0;const badge=tab.querySelector('small');if(badge)badge.textContent=String(n);});
      }
    }
    state.syncItemFolderCard=slot=>{
      if(state.currentRoom!==room||!state.modal)return;
      const card=[...state.modal.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-item-slots .rpcm-slot[data-slot-id]')].find(node=>node.dataset.slotId===String(slot.id));
      if(card)moveSlotFolderInPlace(card,slot);
    };

    function createSlotCard(slot, openDefault = false) {
      const d = document.createElement('details');
      const inlineRetention = slot.group === 'character' || slot.group === 'item' || slot.group === 'extra' || slot.id === 'currentState';
      const fallbackTitle = slot.group === 'character' ? '새 캐릭터' : slot.group === 'item' ? '중요 물품' : '기타';
      const automaticTerms = slot.group === 'character' ? characterAutomaticTerms(slot) : slot.group === 'item' ? importantItemAutomaticTerms(slot) : [];
      const initialGuideVariant = slot.id === 'logSummary' ? getLogSummaryGuideVariant(false) : '';
      const initialGuideModified = !!BASE_GUIDES[slot.id] && isGuideTextModified(slot.id, initialGuideVariant);
      const itemFolderOptions = slot.group === 'item' ? importantItemFolderNames(room) : [];
      const itemHeader = slot.group === 'item' ? importantItemHeaderParts(slot.title) : null;
      const itemChildCount = slot.group === 'item' ? parseImportantItemReadBody(slot.content).children.length : 0;
      const itemFolderListId = `rpcm-item-folder-options-${String(slot.id).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g, '')}`;
      const itemSettingsPanelId = `rpcm-item-settings-${String(slot.id).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g, '')}`;
      const extraCategoryListId = `rpcm-extra-category-options-${String(slot.id).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g, '')}`;
      const extraCategoryNames = [...new Set(room.slots.filter(s => s.group === 'extra').map(s => String(s.extraCategory || '').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
      d.className = `rpcm-slot${inlineRetention ? ' rpcm-slot-inline-retention' : ''}`;
      d.dataset.slotId = slot.id;
      d.open = hadPreviousRender ? previouslyOpenSlots.has(String(slot.id)) : openDefault;
      const deletable = slot.group === 'character' || slot.group === 'item' || slot.group === 'extra';
      const titleEditable = deletable;
      const pendingItem = pending?.items?.find(i => i.slotId === slot.id && (Number(i.totalTurns || 0) === 0 || Number(i.usedTurns || 0) < Number(i.totalTurns || 0)));
      d.innerHTML = `
        <summary>
          ${['character', 'item', 'extra'].includes(slot.group) ? '<span class="rpcm-drag-handle" draggable="true" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="드래그하여 항목 순서 또는 폴더 이동" title="드래그하여 이동">⋮⋮</span>' : ''}
          <input class="rpcm-enable" type="checkbox" ${slot.enabled ? 'checked' : ''} title="${slot.group === 'character' || slot.group === 'item' ? '현재 주입 여부 · 자동 감지 대상 지정이 아닙니다.' : '현재 주입 여부'}" aria-label="${esc(slot.title)} 현재 주입 여부">
          ${slot.group === 'item' ? `<label class="rpcm-item-delete-label" title="삭제할 물품 선택 · 주입 여부에는 영향 없음"><input class="rpcm-item-delete-select" type="checkbox" ${previouslySelectedItems.has(String(slot.id)) ? 'checked' : ''} aria-label="${esc(slot.title)} 삭제 선택"><span>삭제 선택</span></label>` : ''}
          <span class="rpcm-slot-name">${slot.id === 'currentState' ? '🧭 ' : slot.id === 'logSummary' ? '🗓️ ' : slot.group === 'item' ? '📦 ' : ''}${esc(slot.group === 'item' ? itemHeader.name : slot.title)}</span>
          ${slot.group === 'item' ? `<span class="rpcm-item-head-meta">${itemHeader.date ? `최초 ${esc(itemHeader.date)}` : '최초 날짜 미기록'} · 하위 ${itemChildCount}개</span>` : ''}
          ${slot.group === 'item' ? `<button type="button" class="rpcm-slot-quick-edit" aria-label="${esc(slot.title)} 바로 편집">편집</button>` : ''}
          ${pendingItem ? `<span class="rpcm-slot-remain">${esc(remainingLabelForItem(pendingItem))}</span>` : ''}
          ${inlineRetention && slot.group !== 'item' ? `<label class="rpcm-inline-retention" title="호출 후 앞으로 몇 번의 AI 응답에 연속 주입할지 선택 · 주기 반복 아님"><span>연속 유지</span><select class="rpcm-slot-retention" aria-label="${esc(slot.title)} 연속 유지 턴">${retentionOptionsHtml(slot.retentionTurns)}</select></label>` : ''}
          ${BASE_GUIDES[slot.id] ? `<button class="rpcm-guide-toggle${initialGuideModified ? ' is-modified' : ''}" type="button" title="GPT/Gemini에 복사해 쓸 수 있는 업데이트 지침">지침</button>` : ''}

          <span class="rpcm-slot-count" ${slot.group === 'item' ? 'hidden' : ''}>${formatCount(String(slot.content || '').length)}자</span>
          ${deletable ? `<button class="rpcm-delete-btn" type="button">삭제</button>` : ''}
          <span class="rpcm-chevron">▶</span>
        </summary>
        ${slot.group === 'fixed' ? '<div class="rpcm-slot-readview"><div class="rpcm-read-toolbar"><span>저장된 내용을 블록으로 표시합니다.</span><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary rpcm-raw-edit-toggle">원문 편집</button></div><div class="rpcm-read-preview"></div></div>' : ''}
        ${slot.group === 'item' ? `<div class="rpcm-item-readview"><div class="rpcm-item-view-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-utility-shortcut="related" data-utility-ref="item:${esc(slot.id)}">관련 기억</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-utility-shortcut="source" data-utility-ref="item:${esc(slot.id)}">근거 찾기</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-utility-shortcut="trigger" data-utility-ref="item:${esc(slot.id)}">AI 별칭</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary rpcm-item-edit-toggle">편집</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary rpcm-item-settings-toggle" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="${esc(itemSettingsPanelId)}">설정</button></div><div class="rpcm-item-settings-panel" id="${esc(itemSettingsPanelId)}" hidden><div class="rpcm-item-settings-body"><label class="rpcm-inline-retention"><span>연속 유지</span><select class="rpcm-slot-retention" aria-label="${esc(slot.title)} 연속 유지 턴">${retentionOptionsHtml(slot.retentionTurns)}</select></label></div></div><div class="rpcm-item-read-preview"></div></div>` : ''}
        <div class="rpcm-edit" ${slot.group === 'fixed' || slot.group === 'item' ? 'hidden' : ''}>
          ${slot.group === 'item' ? '<div class="rpcm-item-editor-head"><strong>물품 원문 편집</strong><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary rpcm-item-edit-done">읽기 화면</button></div>' : ''}
          ${titleEditable ? `<input class="rpcm-title-input" value="${esc(slot.title)}" placeholder="${slot.group === 'item' ? '물품 이름 (예: 폴라로이드 사진 묶음)' : '항목 이름'}">` : `<div class="rpcm-fixed-note">${slot.id === 'currentState' ? '기준 시점·진행 단계·미완료 후크·지속 부상·합의·제약 등 방 전체의 현재값을 넣습니다. 인물별 인지·호칭·말투·관계선은 인물정보 탭에서 관리합니다.' : '날짜별 사건 요약 전체를 붙여넣습니다. 원문은 저장소로 보관하고, 날짜 블록 단위로 분해해 직접 주입·최근·관련·항상 주입 날짜만 골라 주입합니다. 최근·관련 로그 자동 선택을 꺼도 직접 주입·항상 주입 날짜는 유지됩니다.'}</div>${BASE_GUIDES[slot.id] ? `<div class="rpcm-guide-panel" hidden><div class="rpcm-guide-head"><span>GPT / Gemini용 업데이트 지침 · ${slot.id === 'logSummary' ? '종류별 수정 내용' : '수정 내용'}은 이 브라우저에 자동 저장됩니다.</span>${slot.id === 'logSummary' ? '<select class="rpcm-guide-variant" aria-label="날짜요약 지침 종류"><option value="general">일반용</option><option value="adult">성인용</option></select>' : ''}<button class="rpcm-guide-icon" type="button" data-guide-copy title="지침 복사" aria-label="지침 복사">${GUIDE_COPY_ICON}</button><button class="rpcm-guide-reset" type="button" data-guide-reset>기본값 복원</button></div><textarea class="rpcm-guide-textarea" data-rpcm-editor="true" spellcheck="false"></textarea></div>` : ''}`}
          ${slot.group === 'item' ? `<label class="rpcm-item-folder-field"><span>폴더</span><input class="rpcm-item-folder-input" list="${esc(itemFolderListId)}" value="${esc(slot.itemFolder || '')}" placeholder="폴더 이름 입력 또는 기존 폴더 선택"><datalist id="${esc(itemFolderListId)}">${itemFolderOptions.map(name => `<option value="${esc(name)}"></option>`).join('')}</datalist></label>` : ''}
          ${slot.group === 'extra' ? `<label class="rpcm-extra-category-field"><span>카테고리</span><input class="rpcm-extra-category-input" list="${esc(extraCategoryListId)}" value="${esc(slot.extraCategory || '')}" placeholder="예: OOC · 문체 · 출력 규칙 (비우면 미분류)"><datalist id="${esc(extraCategoryListId)}">${extraCategoryNames.map(name => `<option value="${esc(name)}"></option>`).join('')}</datalist></label>` : ''}

      const ta = d.querySelector('.rpcm-textarea');
      const count = d.querySelector('.rpcm-slot-count');
      const name = d.querySelector('.rpcm-slot-name');
      const titleInput = d.querySelector('.rpcm-title-input');
      const itemFolderInput = d.querySelector('.rpcm-item-folder-input');

        picker.onchange=()=>{if(!picker.value)return;folderInput.value=picker.value;folderInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));};
        folderInput.closest('label')?.appendChild(picker);
      }
      const saveDone=document.createElement('button');
      saveDone.type='button';saveDone.className='rpcm-editor-action rpcm-slot-save-done';saveDone.textContent='저장·완료';
      d.querySelector('.rpcm-editor-actions')?.appendChild(saveDone);
      saveDone.onclick=async()=>{
        if(saveDone.disabled)return;
        saveDone.disabled=true;saveDone.textContent='저장 중…';
        try{
          if(titleInput){slot.title=titleInput.value.trim()||slot.title;name.textContent=slot.group==='item'?`📦 ${importantItemHeaderParts(slot.title).name}`:slot.title;}

          await saveRoom(room,'editor-save-done');

          else d.open=false;
          notify('저장했습니다.','success');
        }catch(error){updateSaveStatusUi('error');notify(`저장 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}
        finally{saveDone.disabled=false;saveDone.textContent='저장·완료';}
      };

          overlay.dataset.rpcmDragId = String(slot.id);

        });
        dragHandle.addEventListener('dragend', () => {
          delete overlay.dataset.rpcmDragId;
          delete overlay.dataset.rpcmDragGroup;
          d.classList.remove('rpcm-dragging');
          overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-drop-target').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('rpcm-drop-target'));
        });

      let activeGuideVariant = initialGuideVariant;

      ta.defaultValue = slot.content || '';
      ta.value = slot.content || '';
      if (slot.group === 'item') {
        const settings = d.querySelector('.rpcm-item-settings-body');
        [d.querySelector('.rpcm-item-folder-field'),
         d.querySelector('.rpcm-item-advanced'), d.querySelector('.rpcm-delete-btn')]
          .filter(Boolean).forEach(node => settings.appendChild(node));
        const settingsPanel = d.querySelector('.rpcm-item-settings-panel');
        const settingsToggle = d.querySelector('.rpcm-item-settings-toggle');
        const showItemSettings = show => {
          settingsPanel.hidden = !show;
          settingsToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(show));
        };
        showItemSettings(previouslyOpenItemSettings.has(String(slot.id)));
        settingsToggle.onclick = () => showItemSettings(settingsPanel.hidden);

        const paintItem = () => {
          const header = importantItemHeaderParts(slot.title);

          name.textContent = `📦 ${header.name}`;
          const meta = d.querySelector('.rpcm-item-head-meta');
          if (meta) meta.textContent = `${header.date ? '최초 ' + header.date : '최초 날짜 미기록'} · 하위 ${children.length}개`;
          itemPreview.innerHTML = renderImportantItemReadBody(ta.value);
        };
        paintItem();
        const showItemEditor = show => {
          itemEditor.hidden = !show;
          itemReadView.hidden = show;
          if (!show) paintItem();
          if (show) { showItemSettings(false); d.open = true; if (!isMobileManagerLayout()) ta.focus(); }
        };
        showItemEditor(previouslyRawOpen.has(String(slot.id)));
        d.querySelector('.rpcm-item-edit-toggle').onclick = () => showItemEditor(true);
        d.querySelector('.rpcm-slot-quick-edit').onclick = event => { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); showItemEditor(true); };
        d.querySelector('.rpcm-item-edit-done').onclick = () => showItemEditor(false);
        itemPreview.onclick = event => {
          const button = event.target.closest('[data-item-child-edit]');
          if (!button) return;
          const parsed = parseImportantItemReadBody(ta.value);
          const child = parsed.children[Number(button.dataset.itemChildEdit)];
          if (!child) return;
          const card = button.closest('.rpcm-item-child');
          const oldBody = parsed.raw.slice(child.headerEnd, child.end);
          const description = importantItemVisibleText(oldBody);
          const editor = document.createElement('div');
          editor.className = 'rpcm-item-child-editor';
          editor.innerHTML = `<label>하위 항목 이름<input class="rpcm-child-name" value="${esc(child.name)}"></label>
            <label>보관자<input class="rpcm-child-keeper" value="${esc(child.keeper)}" placeholder="미확정이면 원문 그대로"></label>
            <label>설명<textarea class="rpcm-child-description" rows="5"></textarea></label>
            <div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-child-cancel>취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-child-save>저장</button></div>`;
          editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-description').value = description;
          card.appendChild(editor);
          button.hidden = true;
          editor.querySelector('[data-child-cancel]').onclick = () => { editor.remove(); button.hidden = false; };
          editor.querySelector('[data-child-save]').onclick = () => {
            const newName = editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-name').value.trim();
            if (!newName) { notify('하위 항목 이름을 입력해 주세요.', 'warn'); return; }
            const updated = editImportantItemChild(ta.value, Number(button.dataset.itemChildEdit), newName,
              editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-keeper').value,
              editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-description').value);
            if (updated === null) { notify('하위 항목을 다시 열어 주세요.', 'warn'); return; }
            ta.value = updated;
            ta.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles:true }));
            paintItem();
          };
          editor.querySelector('.rpcm-child-name').focus();
        };
        ta.addEventListener('input', paintItem);
        titleInput?.addEventListener('input', () => { slot.title = titleInput.value; paintItem(); });
      }
      const readView = d.querySelector('.rpcm-slot-readview');
      const readPreview = d.querySelector('.rpcm-read-preview');
      const rawEditor = d.querySelector('.rpcm-edit');
      const rawToggle = d.querySelector('.rpcm-raw-edit-toggle');
      const paintReadView = () => {
        if (readPreview) readPreview.innerHTML = renderFixedSlotReadView(slot, ta.value, room);
      };
      if (readView) {
        paintReadView();
        readView.addEventListener('click', async event => {
          const copyButton = event.target.closest('[data-read-copy]');
          if (!copyButton) return;
          event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
          const parsed = fixedSlotReadBlocks(slot, ta.value);
          const item = parsed.blocks[Number(copyButton.dataset.readCopy)];
          if (!item) return;
          const copied = await copyPlainText(item.raw);
          notify(copied ? '블록 원문을 복사했습니다.' : '블록 복사에 실패했습니다.', copied ? 'success' : 'error', 2800);
        });
        const openRaw = previouslyRawOpen.has(String(slot.id));
        rawEditor.hidden = !openRaw;
        readPreview.hidden = openRaw;
        rawToggle.textContent = openRaw ? '블록 보기' : '원문 편집';
        rawToggle.onclick = () => {
          const showRaw = rawEditor.hidden;
          if (!showRaw) paintReadView();
          rawEditor.hidden = !showRaw;
          readPreview.hidden = showRaw;
          rawToggle.textContent = showRaw ? '블록 보기' : '원문 편집';
          if (showRaw) { d.open = true; ta.focus(); }
        };
      }
      const refreshItemTemplateExample = () => {

      let committedLogText = slot.id === 'logSummary' ? String(ta.value || '') : '';
      const commitLogTimeline = () => {
        if (slot.id !== 'logSummary') return ta.value;
        const next = alignNewManualLogBlocksToActiveTimeline(committedLogText, ta.value, room);
        committedLogText = next;
        if (next !== ta.value) {
          ta.value = next;
          ta.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles:true }));
        }
        slot.content = next;
        return next;
      };
      if (slot.id === 'logSummary') {
        ta._rpcmCommitLogTimeline = commitLogTimeline;
        ta.addEventListener('blur', commitLogTimeline);
      }
      const commitTextareaValue = () => {
        ta.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles:true }));
      };
      if (['currentState', 'logSummary'].includes(slot.id)) {
        ta.addEventListener('pointerup', () => { if (!isMobileManagerLayout()) rememberEditorHeight(slot, ta); });
        ta.addEventListener('keyup', event => { if (!isMobileManagerLayout() && (event.key === 'ArrowUp' || event.key === 'ArrowDown')) rememberEditorHeight(slot, ta); });
      }
      // 현재상태/로그요약은 한 번 클릭해 포커스된 동안에만 textarea 내부를 스크롤합니다.
      // 포커스되지 않은 축소 편집창 위에서는 휠 입력을 프레임 단위로 합산해
      // 실제 스크롤 영역으로 넘겨 고해상도 휠/터치패드의 과도한 동기 scrollTop 갱신을 줄입니다.
      if (['currentState', 'logSummary'].includes(slot.id)) {
        let wheelFrame = 0;
        let pendingWheelX = 0;
        let pendingWheelY = 0;
        ta.addEventListener('wheel', event => {
          const scrollHost = state.modal?.querySelector(isMobileManagerLayout() ? '.rpcm-body' : '.rpcm-work-content');
          if (!scrollHost || document.querySelector('#rpcm-detached-backdrop')) return;
          if (document.activeElement === ta) return;
          event.preventDefault();
          const wheelScale = event.deltaMode === 1 ? 18 : event.deltaMode === 2 ? Math.max(320, scrollHost.clientHeight) : 1;
          pendingWheelY += event.deltaY * wheelScale;
          pendingWheelX += event.deltaX * wheelScale;
          if (wheelFrame) return;
          wheelFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            wheelFrame = 0;
            const liveBody = state.modal?.querySelector(isMobileManagerLayout() ? '.rpcm-body' : '.rpcm-work-content');
            const deltaY = pendingWheelY;
            const deltaX = pendingWheelX;
            pendingWheelY = 0;
            pendingWheelX = 0;
            if (!liveBody || document.querySelector('#rpcm-detached-backdrop')) return;
            liveBody.scrollTop += deltaY;
